We study reasoning through two such primitives, recall and state-tracking, and ask whether hybrid architectures that combine attention-based retrieval with recurrent state updates are better suited than attention-only models for tasks that jointly require both. Using matched Olmo3 transformer and hybrid models in instruction-tuned and reasoning-augmented variants, we evaluate these models on a set of controlled tasks involving a mixture of state-tracking and recall primitives, state-based recall. Across tasks, we notice that reasoning augmentation provides the largest overall improvement, substantially extending the range of difficulty over which models remain effective. We also notice that in certain tasks, the hybrid reasoning model remains substantially more robust as sequential dependence increases. In contrast, the transformer reasoning model degrades sharply in performance as task difficulty increases beyond a given threshold. These results suggest that reasoning tokens and architectural inductive biases contribute at different levels of the computational process: explicit reasoning can expand a model's effective operating range, but its benefit depends on how well the underlying architecture supports persistent state propagation. Given the small size of our case study, which involves a limited set of models and tasks, we present these findings as suggestive rather than conclusive and leave broader validation across model families, scales, and task variations to future work.

</details>


### [99] [Cross-Domain Data Selection and Augmentation for Automatic Compliance Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21469)
*Fariz Ikhwantri,Dusica Marijan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究通过有选择性地从源域选取增量数据，在自然语言推理框架下提升法规合规检测的跨领域迁移能力，有效减少了负迁移现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法规文本复杂多变，现有合规检测模型在不同法规间泛化能力差，负迁移问题突出，亟需一种原则性的跨领域迁移方法。

Method: 将合规检测建模为自然语言推理（NLI）任务，比较四种数据选择策略（随机采样、Moore-Lewis交叉熵差异、重要性加权、嵌入检索），通过调整选取数据比例系统分析其对跨领域适应的影响。

Result: 目标性数据选择能够显著减少负迁移，不同选择策略和选取比例对迁移效果有显著影响。

Conclusion: 数据选择是提升法规合规检测跨领域泛化的有效路径，为实现跨异构法规的可扩展、可靠自动化合规检测提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Automating the detection of regulatory compliance remains a challenging task due to the complexity and variability of legal texts. Models trained on one regulation often fail to generalise to others. This limitation underscores the need for principled methods to improve cross-domain transfer. We study data selection as a strategy to mitigate negative transfer in compliance detection framed as a natural language inference (NLI) task. Specifically, we evaluate four approaches for selecting augmentation data from a larger source domain: random sampling, Moore-Lewis's cross-entropy difference, importance weighting, and embedding-based retrieval. We systematically vary the proportion of selected data to analyse its effect on cross-domain adaptation. Our findings demonstrate that targeted data selection substantially reduces negative transfer, offering a practical path toward scalable and reliable compliance automation across heterogeneous regulations.

</details>


### [100] [Preferences of a Voice-First Nation: Large-Scale Pairwise Evaluation and Preference Analysis for TTS in Indian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21481)
*Srija Anand,Ashwin Sankar,Ishvinder Sethi,Aaditya Pareek,Kartik Rajput,Gaurav Yadav,Nikhil Narasimhan,Adish Pandya,Deepon Halder,Mohammed Safi Ur Rahman Khan,Praveen S,Shobhit Banga,Mitesh M Khapra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多语言文本转语音（TTS）系统的受控多维成对评估框架，在10种印度语言上使用5000+句子、120K+成对比较和1900+评审者，从6个感知维度（可懂度、表现力、语音质量、生动性、噪声、幻觉）进行评估，并基于Bradley-Terry模型构建了多语言排行榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众包成对评估虽可扩展，但应用于TTS时会因语言多样性和语音感知的多维性导致高方差，缺乏对多语言场景下感知维度的精细控制。

Method: 提出受控多维成对评估框架：结合语言控制（5K+原生和语码混合句子，覆盖10种印度语言）与感知导向标注（6个维度）。收集7个SOTA TTS系统的120K+成对比较，由1900+母语评审者完成。使用Bradley-Terry模型构建排行榜，SHAP分析解释偏好，并分析排行榜可靠性及模型在各感知维度的优势与权衡。

Result: 构建了多语言TTS排行榜，通过SHAP分析揭示了人类偏好的可解释性，展示了模型在不同感知维度上的表现权衡与强度，验证了排行榜的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该受控多维成对评估框架能够有效降低多语言TTS评估中的方差，提供细粒度的感知维度分析，为多语言TTS系统的标准化评估提供了可靠方法。

Abstract: Crowdsourced pairwise evaluation has emerged as a scalable approach for assessing foundation models. However, applying it to Text to Speech(TTS) introduces high variance due to linguistic diversity and multidimensional nature of speech perception. We present a controlled multidimensional pairwise evaluation framework for multilingual TTS that combines linguistic control with perceptually grounded annotation. Using 5K+ native and code-mixed sentences across 10 Indic languages, we evaluate 7 state-of-the-art TTS systems and collect over 120K pairwise comparisons from over 1900 native raters. In addition to overall preference, raters provide judgments across 6 perceptual dimensions: intelligibility, expressiveness, voice quality, liveliness, noise, and hallucinations. Using Bradley-Terry modeling, we construct a multilingual leaderboard, interpret human preference using SHAP analysis and analyze leaderboard reliability alongside model strengths and trade-offs across perceptual dimensions.

</details>


### [101] [OptiVerse: A Comprehensive Benchmark towards Optimization Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21510)
*Xinyu Zhang,Boxuan Zhang,Yuchen Wan,Lingling Zhang,YiXing Yao,Bifan Wei,Yaqiang Wu,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OptiVerse是一个包含1000个优化问题的基准测试集，涵盖随机优化、动态优化、博弈优化和最优控制等被忽视的领域。实验表明，即使在GPT-5.2和Gemini-3等先进模型上，难题准确率也低于27%，主要瓶颈在于建模与逻辑错误。为此提出双视角审计智能体（Dual-View Auditor Agent）来提升LLM建模过程的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化基准测试过度聚焦于数学规划和组合优化，忽略了随机优化、动态优化、博弈优化和最优控制等关键领域，无法全面评估LLM在复杂优化任务上的能力。

Method: 构建了包含1000个精心策划问题的综合基准OptiVerse，涵盖四个被忽视的优化领域，设置Easy、Medium、Hard三个难度级别；并提出Dual-View Auditor Agent来改进LLM建模过程的准确性。

Result: 对22个不同规模的LLM进行实验，发现难题准确率严重下降，GPT-5.2和Gemini-3等先进模型也难以超过27%的准确率；错误分析表明建模与逻辑错误是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: OptiVerse为推进LLM解决复杂优化挑战提供了基础平台，双视角审计智能体能在不显著增加时间开销的情况下提升建模准确性。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable reasoning, complex optimization tasks remain challenging, requiring domain knowledge and robust implementation. However, existing benchmarks focus narrowly on Mathematical Programming and Combinatorial Optimization, hindering comprehensive evaluation. To address this, we introduce OptiVerse, a comprehensive benchmark of 1,000 curated problems spanning neglected domains, including Stochastic Optimization, Dynamic Optimization, Game Optimization, and Optimal Control, across three difficulty levels: Easy, Medium, and Hard. The experiments with 22 LLMs of different sizes reveal sharp performance degradation on hard problems, where even advanced models like GPT-5.2 and Gemini-3 struggle to exceed 27% accuracy. Through error analysis, we identify that modeling & logic errors remain the primary bottleneck. Consequently, we propose a Dual-View Auditor Agent that improves the accuracy of the LLM modeling process without introducing significant time overhead. OptiVerse will serve as a foundational platform for advancing LLMs in solving complex optimization challenges.

</details>


### [102] [UKP_Psycontrol at SemEval-2026 Task 2: Modeling Valence and Arousal Dynamics from Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21534)
*Darya Hryhoryeva,Amaia Zurinaga,Hamidreza Jamalabadi,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对SemEval-2026 Task 2中用户生成文本的当前情感与短期情感变化建模任务，提出三种互补方法：LLM提示（考虑/不考虑用户信息）、基于Ising交互的成对最大熵模型进行结构化的状态转移建模、以及融合近期情感轨迹与可训练用户嵌入的轻量级神经回归模型。实验表明LLM擅长捕捉静态情感信号，而短期情感变化更由近期数值轨迹解释。系统在Subtask 1和Subtask 2A中均排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于时间序列的用户生成文本中，不仅需要建模当前时刻的情感状态，还需捕捉情感在短期内的动态变化，这对情感计算和心理健康监测等应用具有重要意义。

Method: 三种互补方法：(1) LLM提示（user-aware与user-agnostic设置）；(2) 基于Ising型交互的成对最大熵模型用于结构化状态转移建模；(3) 融合近期情感轨迹与可训练用户嵌入的轻量级神经回归模型。

Result: LLM有效捕捉静态情感信号；短期情感变化更由近期数值轨迹（而非文本语义）解释。系统在Subtask 1和Subtask 2A的官方评测指标上均排名第一。

Conclusion: 文本语义（LLM）擅长静态情感建模，而时序数值轨迹对短期情感变化建模更为关键，三种方法的互补组合可实现最优性能。

Abstract: This paper presents our system developed for SemEval-2026 Task 2. The task requires modeling both current affect and short-term affective change in chronologically ordered user-generated texts. We explore three complementary approaches: (1) LLM prompting under user-aware and user-agnostic settings, (2) a pairwise Maximum Entropy (MaxEnt) model with Ising-style interactions for structured transition modeling, and (3) a lightweight neural regression model incorporating recent affective trajectories and trainable user embeddings. Our findings indicate that LLMs effectively capture static affective signals from text, whereas short-term affective variation in this dataset is more strongly explained by recent numeric state trajectories than by textual semantics. Our system ranked first among participating teams in both Subtask 1 and Subtask 2A based on the official evaluation metric.

</details>


### [103] [Finding Meaning in Embeddings: Concept Separation Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21555)
*Paul Keuren,Marc Ponsen,Robert Ayoub Bagheri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需分类器的句子嵌入质量评估方法——概念分离曲线（Concept Separation Curves），通过系统性地向句子引入句法噪声和语义否定，量化其对嵌入的影响，从而客观评估模型捕捉句子概念的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有句子嵌入评估方法依赖额外的分类器或下游任务，导致难以区分良好结果源自嵌入本身还是分类器行为，因此需要一种与分类器无关的客观评估方法。

Method: 系统性地向句子引入句法噪声和语义否定，量化其对嵌入向量的影响，并利用概念分离曲线可视化模型区分概念变化与表层变化的能力。

Result: 通过多领域、跨语言（荷兰语和英语）及不同句子长度的实验，证明概念分离曲线能够提供可解释、可复现、跨模型的句子嵌入概念稳定性评估。

Conclusion: 概念分离曲线是一种有效的、分类器无关的句子嵌入评估工具，能够客观衡量嵌入模型对句子核心概念的捕捉能力。

Abstract: Sentence embedding techniques aim to encode key concepts of a sentence's meaning in a vector space. However, the majority of evaluation approaches for sentence embedding quality rely on the use of additional classifiers or downstream tasks. These additional components make it unclear whether good results stem from the embedding itself or from the classifier's behaviour. In this paper, we propose a novel method for evaluating the effectiveness of sentence embedding methods in capturing sentence-level concepts. Our approach is classifier-independent, allowing for an objective assessment of the model's performance. The approach adopted in this study involves the systematic introduction of syntactic noise and semantic negations into sentences, with the subsequent quantification of their relative effects on the resulting embeddings. The visualisation of these effects is facilitated by Concept Separation Curves, which show the model's capacity to differentiate between conceptual and surface-level variations. By leveraging data from multiple domains, employing both Dutch and English languages, and examining sentence lengths, this study offers a compelling demonstration that Concept Separation Curves provide an interpretable, reproducible, and cross-model approach for evaluating the conceptual stability of sentence embeddings.

</details>


### [104] [AgenticQwen: Training Small Agentic Language Models with Dual Data Flywheels for Industrial-Scale Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21590)
*Yuanjie Lyu,Chengyu Wang,Haonan Zheng,Yuanhao Yue,Junbing Yan,Ming Wang,Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticQwen系列模型，通过多轮强化学习（结合推理RL和智能体RL）与双数据飞轮（推理飞轮通过错误学习增加难度，智能体飞轮将线性流程扩展为多分支行为树）进行训练，在公共基准和工业智能体系统中均取得强性能，缩小了与更大模型的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代工业应用对具备多步推理和工具使用能力的小型智能体模型有强烈需求，以在严格的成本和延迟约束下运行。

Method: 采用多轮强化学习框架，结合推理RL和智能体RL；设计双数据飞轮机制：推理飞轮从错误中学习并逐步增加任务难度，智能体飞轮将线性工作流扩展为多分支行为树以反映真实决策复杂性。

Result: 在多个智能体基准测试上表现强劲；在工业智能体系统中，搜索和数据分析任务上缩小了与更大模型的性能差距。

Conclusion: AgenticQwen系列模型通过创新的强化学习框架和双数据飞轮，有效提升了小型模型在智能体任务上的能力，在实际工业部署中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Modern industrial applications increasingly demand language models that act as agents, capable of multi-step reasoning and tool use in real-world settings. These tasks are typically performed under strict cost and latency constraints, making small agentic models highly desirable. In this paper, we introduce the AgenticQwen family of models, trained via multi-round reinforcement learning (RL) on synthetic data and a limited amount of open-source data. Our training framework combines reasoning RL and agentic RL with dual data flywheels that automatically generate increasingly challenging tasks. The reasoning flywheel increases task difficulty by learning from errors, while the agentic flywheel expands linear workflows into multi-branch behavior trees that better reflect the decision complexity of real-world applications. We validate AgenticQwen on public benchmarks and in an industrial agent system. The models achieve strong performance on multiple agentic benchmarks, and in our industrial agent system, close the gap with much larger models on search and data analysis tasks. Model checkpoints and part of the synthetic data: https://huggingface.co/collections/alibaba-pai/agenticqwen. Data synthesis and RL training code: https://github.com/haruhi-sudo/data_synth_and_rl. The data synthesis pipeline is also integrated into EasyDistill: https://github.com/modelscope/easydistill.

</details>


### [105] [Language as a Latent Variable for Reasoning Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21593)
*Linjuan Wu,Haoran Wei,Jialong Tang,Shuang Luo,Baosong Yang,Yongliang Shen,Weiming Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现非英语回答在推理任务上有时比英语更优，并提出将语言视为潜在变量的polyGRPO框架，通过在语言约束和无约束条件下在线生成多语言偏好数据进行强化学习，仅用18.1K数学问题训练即可在多个推理基准上提升6-7%准确率，并展现出跨任务泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到非英语回答在推理任务上有时优于英语回答，提出假设：语言不仅是输出媒介，而是作为潜在变量结构性调控模型的内部推理路径。

Method: 提出polyGRPO（多语言组相对策略优化）框架，将语言变异视为隐式探索信号，在线生成语言约束和无约束条件下的多语言偏好数据，同时对答案准确性和推理结构进行策略优化。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct基础上，仅用18.1K多语言数学问题训练，英语推理测试集绝对准确率提升6.72%，多语言基准提升6.89%，是唯一在纯数学训练下仍能在英语常识推理任务上超越基线的模型（+4.9%）。

Conclusion: 将语言作为潜在变量能够扩展模型的潜在推理空间，带来一致且可泛化的推理性能提升。多语言性拓宽了模型的推理空间，而非仅仅改变输出形式。

Abstract: As LLMs reduce English-centric bias, a surprising trend emerges: non-English responses sometimes outperform English on reasoning tasks. We hypothesize that language functions as a latent variable that structurally modulates the model's internal inference pathways, rather than merely serving as an output medium. To test this, we conducted a Polyglot Thinking Experiment, in which models were prompted to solve identical problems under language-constrained and language-unconstrained conditions. Results show that non-English responses often achieve higher accuracy, and the best performance frequently occur when language is unconstrained, suggesting that multilinguality broadens the model's latent reasoning space. Based on this insight, we propose polyGRPO (Polyglot Group Relative Policy Optimization), an RL framework that treats language variation as an implicit exploration signal. It generates polyglot preference data online under language-constrained and unconstrained conditions, optimizing the policy with respect to both answer accuracy and reasoning structure. Trained on only 18.1K multilingual math problems without chain-of-thought annotations, polyGRPO improves the base model (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct) by 6.72% absolute accuracy on four English reasoning testset and 6.89% in their multilingual benchmark. Remarkably, it is the only method that surpasses the base LLM on English commonsense reasoning task (4.9%), despite being trained solely on math data-highlighting its strong cross-task generalization. Further analysis reveals that treating language as a latent variable expands the model's latent reasoning space, yielding consistent and generalizable improvements in reasoning performance.

</details>


### [106] [Process Supervision via Verbal Critique Improves Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21611)
*Hao-Yuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出言语过程监督（VPS）——一种无需训练的推理时扩展框架，通过强监督者提供结构化自然语言批评来引导迭代生成-批评-修正循环。在GPQA Diamond、AIME 2025和LiveCodeBench V6上，VPS显著提升性能：GPQA达94.9% SOTA，弱模型在AIME上提升高达63.3分，且优于Reflexion和Self-Consistency。批评细粒度被确立为推理时扩展的新轴心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理时扩展集中在链深度（chain depth）、样本广度（sample breadth）和步骤评分器（PRMs）三个维度，缺乏对外部语言监督细粒度的系统研究。本文旨在探索批评细粒度作为第四轴心的有效性。

Method: 提出Verbal Process Supervision (VPS)，一种无需训练（training-free）的框架。使用更强监督者对弱模型的推理过程提供结构化自然语言批评，在给定轮次预算R内迭代执行生成-批评-修正循环。

Result: 1) GPQA Diamond上GPT-5.4 (High)|GPT-5.4 (Low)在R=4时达94.9%，超越SOTA 94.1%。2) AIME 2025上弱模型从11.7-26.7%提升至63.3-90.0%（最高+63.3分）。3) 同等计算量下，VPS优于Reflexion 8.5-12.1分，优于Self-Consistency@5（GPQA +5.0pp，LiveCodeBench +8.3pp）。4) 性能与监督者-演员能力差距呈强正相关（Pearson r=0.90），当错误不可语言表达时（如代码合成）性能下降。

Conclusion: 批评细粒度是推理时扩展的一个新轴心。VPS能有效利用强弱模型之间的言语监督实现显著性能提升，但在代码合成等场景中需要结合言语与可执行方法的混合策略。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling for LLM reasoning has focused on three axes: chain depth, sample breadth, and learned step-scorers (PRMs). We introduce a fourth axis, granularity of external verbal supervision, via Verbal Process Supervision (VPS), a training-free framework that uses structured natural-language critique from a stronger supervisor to guide an iterative generate-critique-refine loop up to a round budget R. Across GPQA Diamond, AIME 2025, and LiveCodeBench V6 (covering both closed and open models), VPS yields three key results. First, on GPQA Diamond, GPT-5.4 (High) | GPT-5.4 (Low) reaches 94.9% at R=4, surpassing the 94.1% state of the art without gradient updates. Second, on AIME 2025, VPS enables strong weak-actor rescue, boosting scores from 11.7-26.7% to 63.3-90.0% (up to +63.3 points). Third, at matched compute, VPS outperforms Reflexion by +8.5 to +12.1 points and Self-Consistency@5 by +5.0 pp (GPQA) and +8.3 pp (LiveCodeBench), isolating critique granularity as the key driver. Performance scales with the supervisor-actor capability gap (Pearson r=0.90) and degrades when errors are not linguistically expressible (e.g., code synthesis), motivating hybrid verbal-executable methods. These results establish critique granularity as a new axis of inference-time scaling.

</details>


### [107] [Multilinguality at the Edge: Developing Language Models for the Global South](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21637)
*Lester James V. Miranda,Songbo Hu,Roi Reichart,Anna Korhonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文调研了232篇论文，分析了在非英语和硬件受限的全球南方社区中，多语言能力与边缘部署（即"最后一公里"问题）相结合的挑战、现状及建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的部署决定了谁能从中受益，但全球南方的非英语和硬件受限社区面临多语言性与边缘部署之间的技术矛盾；这两个领域的研究长期各自为政，缺乏交叉融合。

Method: 对232篇相关论文进行系统调研，覆盖语言建模流水线的各个环节（数据收集、开发、部署），分析多语言与边缘部署结合的技术现状与挑战。

Result: 揭示了多语言性与边缘部署相结合的技术瓶颈，识别了从数据到部署各环节的主要障碍。

Conclusion: 该领域仍存在大量开放问题，作者为NLP生态系统中的不同利益相关者提供了可行的建议，以期推动包容、公平的语言技术发展。

Abstract: Where and how language models (LMs) are deployed determines who can benefit from them. However, there are several challenges that prevent effective deployment of LMs in non-English-speaking and hardware constrained communities in the Global South. We call this challenge the last mile: the intersection of multilinguality and edge deployment, where the goals are aligned but the technical requirements often compete. Studying these two fields together is both a need, as linguistically diverse communities often face the most severe infrastructure constraints, and an opportunity, as edge and multilingual NLP research remain largely siloed. To understand the state of the art and the challenges of combining the two areas, we survey 232 papers that tackle this problem across the language modelling pipeline, from data collection to development and deployment. We also discuss open questions and provide actionable recommendations for different stakeholders in the NLP ecosystem. Finally, we hope that this work contributes to the development of inclusive and equitable language technologies.

</details>


### [108] [Fine-Grained Perspectives: Modeling Explanations with Annotator-Specific Rationales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21667)
*Olufunke O. Sarumi,Charles Welch,Daniel Braun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个联合建模标注者特定标签预测与解释生成的框架，通过User Passport机制编码标注者身份与人口统计信息，并设计了两种解释器架构（事后提示型与前缀桥接型）。实验表明，加入解释建模显著提升预测性能，前缀桥接方法在标签对齐与语义一致性上更优，事后方法在词汇相似性上更强，证明解释建模能更丰富、更忠实地表征标注者间的分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的标注者感知模型仅利用去聚合标签来建模不同视角，忽略了标注者提供的细粒度解释（rationales）中所蕴含的个体视角信号。作者希望利用这些解释来更丰富地表征标注者间的分歧，从而提升预测性能与解释的忠实度。

Method: 基于一个包含去聚合NLI标注与标注者解释的数据集，通过表示层面的User Passport机制将标注者身份和人口统计元数据融入预测条件。提出两种解释器架构：（1）事后提示型解释器（post-hoc prompt-based），在预测后生成解释；（2）前缀桥接型解释器（prefixed bridge），将标注者条件化的分类器表示直接传入生成模型以生成解释。

Result: 引入解释建模显著提升了标注者感知分类器的预测性能。前缀桥接方法在标签对齐与语义一致性上更稳定，事后方法在词汇相似性上更强。

Conclusion: 将解释建模为细粒度视角的表达，能提供更丰富、更忠实的分歧表征。提出的方法将标注者特定的解释同时整合到预测与生成组件中，推进了视角主义建模（perspectivist modeling）的发展。

Abstract: Beyond exploring disaggregated labels for modeling perspectives, annotator rationales provide fine-grained signals of individual perspectives. In this work, we propose a framework for jointly modeling annotator-specific label prediction and corresponding explanations, fine-tuned on the annotators' provided rationales. Using a dataset with disaggregated natural language inference (NLI) annotations and annotator-provided explanations, we condition predictions on both annotator identity and demographic metadata through a representation-level User Passport mechanism. We further introduce two explainer architectures: a post-hoc prompt-based explainer and a prefixed bridge explainer that transfers annotator-conditioned classifier representations directly into a generative model. This design enables explanation generation aligned with individual annotator perspectives. Our results show that incorporating explanation modeling substantially improves predictive performance over a baseline annotator-aware classifier, with the prefixed bridge approach achieving more stable label alignment and higher semantic consistency, while the post-hoc approach yields stronger lexical similarity. These findings indicate that modeling explanations as expressions of fine-grained perspective provides a richer and more faithful representation of disagreement. The proposed approaches advance perspectivist modeling by integrating annotator-specific rationales into both predictive and generative components.

</details>


### [109] [Fixation Sequences as Time Series: A Topological Approach to Dyslexia Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21698)
*Marius Huber,David R. Reich,Lena A. Jäger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将持续同调（持久同调）应用于眼动追踪数据，通过将注视序列解释为时间序列并构建混合模型（融合拓扑特征与传统统计特征），在哥本哈根语料库的阅读障碍检测任务中，混合模型优于纯传统特征方法，且新提出的过滤方法优于现有过滤方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统眼动数据分析主要依赖统计特征，忽视了注视序列中潜在的几何与拓扑结构。持续同调作为拓扑数据分析工具，能从时间序列中提取鲁棒的多尺度特征，有望捕捉传统方法遗漏的互补信息，从而提升阅读障碍检测等任务的性能。

Method: 1) 将注视序列视为时间序列，开发新颖的时间序列过滤方法（filtration）用于持续同调计算；2) 构建混合模型，将拓扑特征与传统统计特征融合；3) 在Copenhagen Corpus数据集上评估，该数据集包含L1和L2读者的阅读障碍与非阅读障碍扫描路径。

Result: 混合模型在阅读障碍检测任务中优于仅使用传统特征的方法；持续同调特征能捕捉注视序列中的互补信息；新提出的过滤方法性能优于现有过滤方法；拓扑特征本身达到了与基线方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: 持续同调为眼动追踪数据分析提供了有价值的补充视角，混合模型策略有效提升了检测性能，未来可推广至更广泛的眼动分析任务。

Abstract: Persistent homology, a method from topological data analysis, extracts robust, multi-scale features from data. It produces stable representations of time series by applying varying thresholds to their values (a process known as a \textit{filtration}). We develop novel filtrations for time series and introduce topological methods for the analysis of eye-tracking data, by interpreting fixation sequences as time series, and constructing ``hybrid models'' that combine topological features with traditional statistical features. We empirically evaluate our method by applying it to the task of dyslexia detection from eye-tracking-while-reading data using the Copenhagen Corpus, which contains scanpaths from dyslexic and non-dyslexic L1 and L2 readers. Our hybrid models outperform existing approaches that rely solely on traditional features, showing that persistent homology captures complementary information encoded in fixation sequences. The strength of these topological features is further underscored by their achieving performance comparable to established baseline methods. Importantly, our proposed filtrations outperform existing ones.

</details>


### [110] [AEL: Agent Evolving Learning for Open-Ended Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21725)
*Wujiang Xu,Jiaojiao Han,Minghao Guo,Kai Mei,Xi Zhu,Han Zhang,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent Evolving Learning（AEL）框架，通过双时间尺度机制（快速：Thompson Sampling选择记忆检索策略；慢速：LLM驱动反思注入因果洞察）解决LLM智能体在开放环境中无法利用历史经验进行自我改进的问题。在组合管理任务上，AEL的夏普比率达2.13±0.47，超越所有基线，且消融实验发现"少即是多"：记忆+反思带来58%提升，但额外机制均导致性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体本质上是无状态的，每个任务都从零开始解决，无法将过往经验转化为更好的未来行为。核心障碍不在于记住什么，而在于如何使用已记住的信息——包括选择何种检索策略、如何解释先前结果、以及何时需要改变当前策略本身。

Method: 提出AEL双时间尺度框架：（1）快速时间尺度：Thompson Sampling bandit学习在每个回合应用哪种记忆检索策略；（2）慢速时间尺度：LLM驱动的反思模块诊断失败模式，将因果洞察注入智能体的决策提示中，为其检索到的证据提供解释框架。

Result: 在10只行业多样股票、208个回合、5个随机种子的序列化组合基准上，AEL实现夏普比率2.13±0.47，优于五种已发表的自我改进方法和所有非LLM基线，且在基于LLM的方法中方差最低。九种消融实验显示"少即是多"：记忆+反思带来58%累积改进，但每增加一种额外机制（规划器进化、工具选择、冷启动初始化、技能提取、三种信用分配方法）均导致性能下降。

Conclusion: 智能体自我改进的瓶颈在于自我诊断如何使用经验，而非增加架构复杂性。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate in open-ended environments spanning hundreds of sequential episodes, yet they remain largely stateless: each task is solved from scratch without converting past experience into better future behavior. The central obstacle is not \emph{what} to remember but \emph{how to use} what has been remembered, including which retrieval policy to apply, how to interpret prior outcomes, and when the current strategy itself must change. We introduce \emph{Agent Evolving Learning} (\ael{}), a two-timescale framework that addresses this obstacle. At the fast timescale, a Thompson Sampling bandit learns which memory retrieval policy to apply at each episode; at the slow timescale, LLM-driven reflection diagnoses failure patterns and injects causal insights into the agent's decision prompt, giving it an interpretive frame for the evidence it retrieves. On a sequential portfolio benchmark (10 sector-diverse tickers, 208 episodes, 5 random seeds), \ael{} achieves a Sharpe ratio of 2.13$\pm$0.47, outperforming five published self-improving methods and all non-LLM baselines while maintaining the lowest variance among all LLM-based approaches. A nine-variant ablation reveals a ``less is more'' pattern: memory and reflection together produce a 58\% cumulative improvement over the stateless baseline, yet every additional mechanism we test (planner evolution, per-tool selection, cold-start initialization, skill extraction, and three credit assignment methods) \emph{degrades} performance. This demonstrates that the bottleneck in agent self-improvement is \emph{self-diagnosing how to use} experience rather than adding architectural complexity. Code and data: https://github.com/WujiangXu/AEL.

</details>


### [111] [Why are all LLMs Obsessed with Japanese Culture? On the Hidden Cultural and Regional Biases of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21751)
*Joseba Fernandez de Landa,Carla Perez-Almendros,Jose Camacho-Collados*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于文化相关开放问题（CROQ）的综合分类数据集，发现LLMs存在对日本等国家的明显文化偏好，且这种偏好出现在监督微调阶段而非预训练阶段；使用英语等高资源语言提示时，模型输出更多样化，对官方语言国家的倾向减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究分析了LLM的文化能力，但缺乏针对文化相关问题时LLM区域偏好的专门研究。

Method: 基于综合分类法构建了文化相关开放问题（CROQ）数据集，通过在不同语言提示下评估LLM的输出，分析其文化偏好，并追踪该偏好出现的训练阶段。

Result: LLM表现出对日本的明显倾向（与以往文化偏见研究结论不同）；使用英语等高资源语言提示时输出更多样化，对官方语言国家倾向减弱；文化偏好的首个明确信号出现在监督微调阶段，而非预训练阶段。

Conclusion: LLM的文化偏好并非源于预训练数据分布，而是主要由监督微调阶段引入，提示语言对文化偏好的表达有显著影响。

Abstract: LLMs have been showing limitations when it comes to cultural coverage and competence, and in some cases show regional biases such as amplifying Western and Anglocentric viewpoints. While there have been works analysing the cultural capabilities of LLMs, there has not been specific work on highlighting LLM regional preferences when it comes to cultural-related questions. In this work, we propose a new dataset based on a comprehensive taxonomy of Culture-Related Open Questions (CROQ). The results show that, contrary to previous cultural bias work, LLMs show a clear tendency towards countries such as Japan. Moveover, our results show that when prompting in languages such as English or other high-resource ones, LLMs tend to provide more diverse outputs and show less inclinations towards answering questions highlighting countries for which the input language is an official language. Finally, we also investigate at which point of LLM training this cultural bias emerges, with our results suggesting that the first clear signs appear after supervised fine-tuning, and not during pre-training.

</details>


### [112] [Misinformation Span Detection in Videos via Audio Transcripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21767)
*Breno Matos,Rennan C. Lima,Savvas Zannettou,Fabricio Benevenuto,Rodrygo L. T. Santos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过创建两个新颖数据集（500+视频，2400+片段），利用音频转录和语言模型实现视频内错误信息片段的检测（F1=0.68），突破了以往仅判断视频整体是否包含错误信息的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频错误信息检测方法仅判断视频整体是否包含错误信息，缺乏对错误信息出现位置和具体内容（即声明）的细粒度识别，导致结果可解释性差。本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 1) 创建两个新数据集，将视频音频转录为文本；2) 标注视频中错误信息声明所在的片段（错误信息跨度检测）；3) 基于最先进的语言模型构建分类器进行检测。

Result: 在超过500个视频、2400+标注片段的数据集上，错误信息片段检测的F1分数达到0.68。数据集、转录文本、音频和视频均已公开。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过音频转录和语言模型实现视频内错误信息细粒度跨度检测的可行性，为视频错误信息检测提供了更具解释性的新范式。

Abstract: Online misinformation is one of the most challenging issues lately, yielding severe consequences, including political polarization, attacks on democracy, and public health risks. Misinformation manifests in any platform with a large user base, including online social networks and messaging apps. It permeates all media and content forms, including images, text, audio, and video. Distinctly, video-based misinformation represents a multifaceted challenge for fact-checkers, given the ease with which individuals can record and upload videos on various video-sharing platforms. Previous research efforts investigated detecting video-based misinformation, focusing on whether a video shares misinformation or not on a video level. While this approach is useful, it only provides a limited and non-easily interpretable view of the problem given that it does not provide an additional context of when misinformation occurs within videos and what content (i.e., claims) are responsible for the video's misinformation nature.
  In this work, we attempt to bridge this research gap by creating two novel datasets that allow us to explore misinformation detection on videos via audio transcripts, focusing on identifying the span of videos that are responsible for the video's misinformation claim (misinformation span detection). We present two new datasets for this task. We transcribe each video's audio to text, identifying the video segment in which the misinformation claims appears, resulting in two datasets of more than 500 videos with over 2,400 segments containing annotated fact-checked claims. Then, we employ classifiers built with state-of-the-art language models, and our results show that we can identify in which part of a video there is misinformation with an F1 score of 0.68. We make publicly available our annotated datasets. We also release all transcripts, audio and videos.

</details>


### [113] [SemEval-2026 Task 4: Narrative Story Similarity and Narrative Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21782)
*Hans Ole Hatzel,Ekaterina Artemova,Haimo Paul Stiemer,Evelyn Gius,Chris Biemann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NSNRL共享任务通过三元组二分类（判断哪个故事与锚点故事更相似）来操作化叙事相似性，收集了1000+故事摘要三元组的人工标注，评估了71个提交系统，发现LLM集成在分类任务中表现最佳，而嵌入任务中预处理/后处理与微调方案性能相当，但两类任务均存在改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叙事相似性缺乏统一的操作化定义，现有工作多依赖直觉或特定领域定义，难以与叙事理论兼容。作者希望建立一种既符合叙事理论又贴近直观判断的相似性概念，并通过标注数据集和评测任务推动叙事表示学习的发展。

Method: 定义叙事相似性为二元分类问题（三元组中哪个故事更相似于锚点故事）；收集1000+故事摘要三元组，每项标注至少2名标注者一致；设置两个评测轨道：三元组分类轨道（判断相似性）和嵌入表示轨道（评估叙事嵌入质量）；接收46个团队的71个最终提交系统。

Result: 三元组分类轨道中，LLM集成系统占据多数最高分；嵌入轨道中，对预训练嵌入模型进行预/后处理的系统与定制微调方案性能相当；两个轨道均存在改进空间；任务网站提供所有团队的嵌入可视化和实例级分类结果。

Conclusion: NSNRL为叙事相似性提供了可操作的定义和基准数据集，实验结果表明当前最佳方案（LLM集成、嵌入预处理/后处理）仍有提升空间，为后续叙事表示学习研究指明了方向。

Abstract: We present the shared task on narrative similarity and narrative representation learning - NSNRL (pronounced "nass-na-rel"). The task operationalizes narrative similarity as a binary classification problem: determining which of two stories is more similar to an anchor story. We introduce a novel definition of narrative similarity, compatible with both narrative theory and intuitive judgment. Based on the similarity judgments collected under this concept, we also evaluate narrative embedding representations. We collected at least two annotations each for more than 1,000 story summary triples, with each annotation being backed by at least two annotators in agreement. This paper describes the sampling and annotation process for the dataset; further, we give an overview of the submitted systems and the techniques they employ. We received a total of 71 final submissions from 46 teams across our two tracks. In our triple-based classification setup, LLM ensembles make up many of the top-scoring systems, while in the embedding setup, systems with pre- and post-processing on pretrained embedding models perform about on par with custom fine-tuned solutions. Our analysis identifies potential headroom for improvement of automated systems in both tracks. The task website includes visualizations of embeddings alongside instance-level classification results for all teams.

</details>


### [114] [Machine Behavior in Relational Moral Dilemmas: Moral Rightness, Predicted Human Behavior, and Model Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21871)
*Jiseon Kim,Jea Kwon,Luiz Felipe Vecchietti,Wenchao Dong,Jaehong Kim,Meeyoung Cha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过举报人困境范式，考察了LLM在不同犯罪严重程度和关系亲密程度下的道德判断。研究发现LLM在道德正确性上保持公平导向，但预测人类行为时会随关系亲密转向忠诚。然而模型自主决策更倾向道德规则而非自身行为预测，揭示了内部世界模型与社会推理之间的不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地用作决策支持系统，但其是否编码了人类道德判断中的社会语境（人际关系、情境依赖）尚不清楚，亟需系统评估。

Method: 采用举报人困境范式，操纵两个实验维度（犯罪严重程度、关系亲密程度），评估LLM在三个视角下的表现：（1）道德正确性（规范性标准）、（2）预测人类行为（描述性社会期望）、（3）自主模型决策。通过分析推理过程进行比较。

Result: 道德正确性始终以公平为导向；预测人类行为随关系亲密程度增加显著转向忠诚；模型自主决策倾向于道德正确性而非自身的行为预测，存在跨视角不一致。

Conclusion: LLM的决策优先考虑僵化的规范性规则，而忽略了其内部世界模型中存在的社会敏感性，这种不一致性可能导致实际部署中的显著错配问题。

Abstract: Human moral judgment is context-dependent and modulated by interpersonal relationships. As large language models (LLMs) increasingly function as decision-support systems, determining whether they encode these social nuances is critical. We characterize machine behavior using the Whistleblower's Dilemma by varying two experimental dimensions: crime severity and relational closeness. Our study evaluates three distinct perspectives: (1) moral rightness (prescriptive norms), (2) predicted human behavior (descriptive social expectations), and (3) autonomous model decision-making. By analyzing the reasoning processes, we identify a clear cross-perspective divergence: while moral rightness remains consistently fairness-oriented, predicted human behavior shifts significantly toward loyalty as relational closeness increases. Crucially, model decisions align with moral rightness judgments rather than their own behavioral predictions. This inconsistency suggests that LLM decision-making prioritizes rigid, prescriptive rules over the social sensitivity present in their internal world-modeling, which poses a gap that may lead to significant misalignments in real-world deployments.

</details>


### [115] [Revisiting Non-Verbatim Memorization in Large Language Models: The Role of Entity Surface Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21882)
*Yuto Nishida,Naoki Shikoda,Yosuke Kishinami,Ryo Fujii,Makoto Morishita,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RedirectQA数据集，通过维基百科重定向信息为实体提供多种表面形式，系统研究了13个LLM中事实知识与表面形式的关系，发现模型预测结果常因表面形式变化而改变，且这种不一致性与表面形式类别相关，事实记忆既非纯粹表面特异也非完全表面不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于实体的问答评估通常使用单一规范表面形式查询每个实体，难以区分事实记忆与通过特定名称的访问，因此需要引入表面形式多样性来更准确地评估LLM的非逐字记忆能力。

Method: 利用维基百科重定向信息，将Wikidata事实三元组与每个实体的分类表面形式（包括别名、缩写、拼写变体和常见错误形式）关联，构建RedirectQA数据集，并在13个LLM上进行表面条件化的事实记忆分析。

Result: 预测结果常因实体表面形式变化而改变，这种不一致性具有类别依赖性：模型对微小拼写变化更鲁棒，对别名和缩写等较大词汇变化更敏感。实体级和表面级频率均与准确率相关，且实体频率的贡献常超过表面频率。

Conclusion: 事实记忆既非纯粹表面特异也非完全表面不变，表面形式多样性对评估非逐字记忆至关重要。

Abstract: Understanding what kinds of factual knowledge large language models (LLMs) memorize is essential for evaluating their reliability and limitations. Entity-based QA is a common framework for analyzing non-verbatim memorization, but typical evaluations query each entity using a single canonical surface form, making it difficult to disentangle fact memorization from access through a particular name. We introduce RedirectQA, an entity-based QA dataset that uses Wikipedia redirect information to associate Wikidata factual triples with categorized surface forms for each entity, including alternative names, abbreviations, spelling variants, and common erroneous forms. Across 13 LLMs, we examine surface-conditioned factual memorization and find that prediction outcomes often change when only the entity surface form changes. This inconsistency is category-dependent: models are more robust to minor orthographic variations than to larger lexical variations such as aliases and abbreviations. Frequency analyses further suggest that both entity- and surface-level frequencies are associated with accuracy, and that entity frequency often contributes beyond surface frequency. Overall, factual memorization appears neither purely surface-specific nor fully surface-invariant, highlighting the importance of surface-form diversity in evaluating non-verbatim memorization.

</details>


### [116] [A Multimodal Text- and Graph-Based Approach for Open-Domain Event Extraction from Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21885)
*Praval Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MODEE，一种结合图学习与LLM文本表示的多模态开放域事件抽取方法，通过建模文档级上下文、结构和语义推理，在开放域和封闭域事件抽取任务上均超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件抽取方法存在局限性：(1)封闭域算法受限于预定义事件类型，难以泛化到未见类型；(2)开放域算法未充分利用大语言模型的能力，且缺乏对文档级上下文、结构和语义推理的显式建模，而LLM本身存在"中间丢失"现象和注意力稀释问题。

Method: 提出多模态开放域事件抽取方法MODEE，将基于图的学习与大语言模型的文本表示相结合，以建模文档级推理过程。

Result: 在大数据集上的实验表明，MODEE在开放域事件抽取任务上优于现有最优方法，并能泛化到封闭域事件抽取，同样超越现有算法。

Conclusion: 图学习与LLM文本表示的结合能有效弥补LLM在文档级推理上的不足，实现跨域事件抽取性能提升。

Abstract: Event extraction is essential for event understanding and analysis. It supports tasks such as document summarization and decision-making in emergency scenarios. However, existing event extraction approaches have limitations: (1) closed-domain algorithms are restricted to predefined event types and thus rarely generalize to unseen types and (2) open-domain event extraction algorithms, capable of handling unconstrained event types, have largely overlooked the potential of large language models (LLMs) despite their advanced abilities. Additionally, they do not explicitly model document-level contextual, structural, and semantic reasoning, which are crucial for effective event extraction but remain challenging for LLMs due to lost-in-the-middle phenomenon and attention dilution. To address these limitations, we propose multimodal open-domain event extraction, MODEE , a novel approach for open-domain event extraction that combines graph-based learning with text-based representation from LLMs to model document-level reasoning. Empirical evaluations on large datasets demonstrate that MODEE outperforms state-of-the-art open-domain event extraction approaches and can be generalized to closed-domain event extraction, where it outperforms existing algorithms.

</details>


### [117] [TingIS: Real-time Risk Event Discovery from Noisy Customer Incidents at Enterprise Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21889)
*Jun Wang,Ziyin Zhang,Rui Wang,Hang Yu,Peng Di,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TingIS是一个端到端的企业级事件发现系统，通过多阶段事件链接引擎（结合高效索引与LLM）、级联路由机制和多维降噪流水线，在峰值吞吐量每分钟2000+消息的生产环境中实现P90告警延迟3.5分钟、高优先级事件发现率95%，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模云原生服务中技术异常的实时检测与缓解至关重要，但客户事件数据存在极端噪声、高吞吐量和语义复杂性问题，现有方法难以从中提取可操作的智能信息。

Method: 提出TingIS系统，核心为多阶段事件链接引擎（融合高效索引与LLM进行事件合并决策），辅以级联路由机制（精确业务归属）和多维降噪流水线（整合领域知识、统计模式和行为过滤）。

Result: 生产环境部署中峰值吞吐量>2000条/分钟、30万条/天；P90告警延迟3.5分钟；高优先级事件发现率95%；在路由准确率、聚类质量和信噪比上显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: TingIS通过结合LLM与传统索引技术，成功在极端噪声和高吞吐场景下实现了高效、精准的企业级事件发现，验证了多阶段链接引擎在云原生环境中的有效性。

Abstract: Real-time detection and mitigation of technical anomalies are critical for large-scale cloud-native services, where even minutes of downtime can result in massive financial losses and diminished user trust. While customer incidents serve as a vital signal for discovering risks missed by monitoring, extracting actionable intelligence from this data remains challenging due to extreme noise, high throughput, and semantic complexity of diverse business lines. In this paper, we present TingIS, an end-to-end system designed for enterprise-grade incident discovery. At the core of TingIS is a multi-stage event linking engine that synergizes efficient indexing techniques with Large Language Models (LLMs) to make informed decisions on event merging, enabling the stable extraction of actionable incidents from just a handful of diverse user descriptions. This engine is complemented by a cascaded routing mechanism for precise business attribution and a multi-dimensional noise reduction pipeline that integrates domain knowledge, statistical patterns, and behavioral filtering. Deployed in a production environment handling a peak throughput of over 2,000 messages per minute and 300,000 messages per day, TingIS achieves a P90 alert latency of 3.5 minutes and a 95\% discovery rate for high-priority incidents. Benchmarks constructed from real-world data demonstrate that TingIS significantly outperforms baseline methods in routing accuracy, clustering quality, and Signal-to-Noise Ratio.

</details>


### [118] [EVENT5Ws: A Large Dataset for Open-Domain Event Extraction from Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21890)
*Praval Sharma,Ashok Samal,Leen-Kiat Soh,Deepti Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文创建了EVENT5Ws，一个大规模、人工标注、统计验证的开放域事件抽取数据集，并通过系统化标注流程和实验评估，证明了该数据集对预训练大语言模型的训练有效性及跨地理环境泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件抽取数据集存在两大局限：闭域环境下事件类型覆盖有限，开放域环境下缺乏大规模人工验证的数据集。这限制了自动化事件抽取方法的发展，尤其是在紧急决策等关键任务中的应用。

Method: 设计了一个系统化的标注流程来构建EVENT5Ws数据集，包含人工标注和统计验证环节；在数据集上评估了多种最新预训练大语言模型，并建立了基准；进一步测试了模型在不同地理环境数据集上的泛化能力。

Result: 成功创建了大规模开放域事件抽取数据集EVENT5Ws；基于该数据集训练的模型能够有效泛化到不同地理环境的数据集上，展现了开发通用算法的潜力。

Conclusion: 总结了数据集开发过程中的经验教训，并为未来大规模数据集开发提供了建议。

Abstract: Event extraction identifies the central aspects of events from text. It supports event understanding and analysis, which is crucial for tasks such as informed decision-making in emergencies. Therefore, it is necessary to develop automated event extraction approaches. However, existing datasets for algorithm development have limitations, including limited coverage of event types in closed-domain settings and a lack of large, manually verified dataset in open-domain settings. To address these limitations, we create EVENT5Ws , a large, manually annotated, and statistically verified open-domain event extraction dataset. We design a systematic annotation pipeline to create the dataset and provide empirical insights into annotation complexity. Using EVENT5Ws, we evaluate state-of-the-art pre-trained large language models and establish a benchmark for future research. We further show that models trained on EVENT5Ws generalize effectively to datasets from different geographical contexts, which demonstrates its potential for developing generalizable algorithms. Finally, we summarize the lessons learned during the dataset development and provide recommendations to support future large-scale dataset development.

</details>


### [119] [Mapping the Political Discourse in the Brazilian Chamber of Deputies: A Multi-Faceted Computational Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21897)
*Flávio Soriano,Victoria F. Mello,Pedro B. Rigueira,Gisele L. Pappa,Wagner Meira,Ana Paula Couto da Silva,Jussara M. Almeida*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合历时文体分析、上下文主题建模和语义聚类的计算框架，对巴西众议院2003-2025年超45万篇演讲进行分析，发现长期风格趋于简短直接、议程随国家危机转向，以及话语联盟中区域和性别身份常比政党 affiliation 更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立法行为分析长期依赖投票记录，忽略了政治话语中丰富的语义和修辞内容，本文旨在弥补这一空白，从"如何说、说什么、谁以相似方式说"三个维度系统分析议会话语。

Method: 提出一个可扩展、可泛化的计算框架，融合三种方法：历时文体分析（diachronic stylometric analysis）、上下文主题建模（contextual topic modeling）和议员演讲语义聚类（semantic clustering）。以巴西众议院2003-2025年超45万篇演讲为语料进行大规模案例研究。

Result: 发现三个主要结果：(1) 长期风格转变：演讲趋于更简短、更直接；(2) 议程转向：立法议程在国家危机时期发生显著转向；(3) 话语联盟：区域和性别身份在话语相似性上比正式政党归属更突出。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了分析议会话语的多维方法论，能够补充传统的基于投票的研究方法，证明话语分析是理解立法行为的有效且互补的途径。

Abstract: Analyses of legislative behavior often rely on voting records, overlooking the rich semantic and rhetorical content of political speech. In this paper, we ask three complementary questions about parliamentary discourse: how things are said, what is being said, and who is speaking in discursively similar ways. To answer these questions, we introduce a scalable and generalizable computational framework that combines diachronic stylometric analysis, contextual topic modeling, and semantic clustering of deputies' speeches. We apply this framework to a large-scale case study of the Brazilian Chamber of Deputies, using a corpus of over 450,000 speeches from 2003 to 2025. Our results show a long-term stylistic shift toward shorter and more direct speeches, a legislative agenda that reorients sharply in response to national crises, and a granular map of discursive alignments in which regional and gender identities often prove more salient than formal party affiliation. More broadly, this work offers a robust methodology for analyzing parliamentary discourse as a multidimensional phenomenon that complements traditional vote-based approaches.

</details>


### [120] [GiVA: Gradient-Informed Bases for Vector-Based Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21901)
*Neeraj Gangwar,Rishabh Deshmukh,Michael Shavlovsky,Hancao Li,Vivek Mittal,Lexing Ying,Nickvash Kani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GiVA，一种基于梯度的初始化策略用于向量式微调方法，在保持极端参数效率的同时，将训练时间降至与LoRA相当，并将所需秩降低8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量式微调方法虽参数效率极高，但需要比LoRA高得多的秩才能达到同等性能，导致训练成本增加。

Method: 提出GiVA（Gradient-based initialization for Vector-based Adaptation），一种基于梯度的初始化策略，用于向量式微调方法。

Result: 在自然语言理解、自然语言生成和图像分类等多个基准测试中，GiVA一致优于或达到与现有向量式微调方法和LoRA竞争的性能，同时将秩需求降低8倍。

Conclusion: GiVA成功结合了向量式微调的极端参数效率和LoRA的低训练成本，实现了高效且经济的模型微调。

Abstract: As model sizes continue to grow, parameter-efficient fine-tuning has emerged as a powerful alternative to full fine-tuning. While LoRA is widely adopted among these methods, recent research has explored vector-based adaptation methods due to their extreme parameter efficiency. However, these methods typically require substantially higher ranks than LoRA to match its performance, leading to increased training costs. This work introduces GiVA, a gradient-based initialization strategy for vector-based adaptation. It achieves training times comparable to LoRA and maintains the extreme parameter efficiency of vector-based adaptation. We evaluate GiVA across diverse benchmarks, including natural language understanding, natural language generation, and image classification. Experiments show that our approach consistently outperforms or achieves performance competitive with existing vector-based adaptation methods and LoRA while reducing rank requirements by a factor of eight ($8\times$).

</details>


### [121] [MathDuels: Evaluating LLMs as Problem Posers and Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21916)
*Zhiqiu Xu,Shibo Jin,Shreya Arya,Mayur Naik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MathDuels，一种自对战基准，让模型同时扮演数学问题的出题者和解题者，通过双角色评估揭示模型能力差异，并通过公开排行榜随新模型发布持续更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学基准评估中，前沿模型在静态问题上已达到近乎天花板的表现，导致评估无法区分模型能力差异，因为这些评估仅将模型视为固定问题集的求解器。

Method: 采用自对战框架，模型同时担任出题者和解题者；通过三阶段生成流水线（元提示、问题生成、难度增强）产生题目，并由独立验证器排除无效问题；使用Rasch模型联合估计解题能力和题目难度。

Result: 在19个前沿模型上的实验表明，出题能力和解题能力部分解耦；双角色评估揭示了单角色基准中无法观察到的能力分离；新模型能产生击败先前主导解题者的问题，基准难度随参与者强度共同进化。

Conclusion: 双角色评估范式能持续区分不断提升的模型能力，避免了静态基准的天花板效应，为模型能力评估提供了可扩展的进化式方案。

Abstract: As frontier language models attain near-ceiling performance on static mathematical benchmarks, existing evaluations are increasingly unable to differentiate model capabilities, largely because they cast models solely as solvers of fixed problem sets. We introduce MathDuels, a self-play benchmark in which models occupy dual roles: each authors math problems under adversarial prompting and solves problems authored by every other participant. Problems are produced through a three-stage generation pipeline (meta-prompting, problem generation, and difficulty amplification), and validated by an independent verifier that excludes ill-posed questions. A Rasch model (Rasch, 1993) jointly estimates solver abilities and problem difficulties; author quality is derived from the difficulties of each model's authored problems. Experiments across 19 frontier models reveal that authoring and solving capabilities are partially decoupled, and that dual-role evaluation reveals capability separations invisible in single-role benchmarks. As newer models enter the arena, they produce problems that defeat previously dominant solvers, so the benchmark's difficulty co-evolves with participant strength rather than saturating at a fixed ceiling. We host a public leaderboard that updates as new models are released.

</details>


### [122] [Evaluation of Automatic Speech Recognition Using Generative Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21928)
*Thibault Bañeras-Roux,Shashi Kumar,Driss Khalil,Sergio Burdisso,Petr Motlicek,Shiran Liu,Mickael Rouvier,Jane Wottawa,Richard Dufour*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探索了解码器型大语言模型（LLM）在ASR语义评估中的应用，通过假设选择、语义距离计算和错误分类三种方法，在HATS数据集上LLM假设选择与人工标注的一致性达92-94%，远超WER的63%，性能优于已有语义指标，且解码器嵌入与编码器模型性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ASR评估指标WER对语义不敏感，而现有基于嵌入的语义指标虽与人类感知相关性更好，但解码器型LLM在该任务上的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 通过三种方法评估LLM：1）在两个候选假设中选择最佳；2）利用生成式嵌入计算语义距离；3）对ASR错误进行定性分类。在HATS数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 最佳LLM在假设选择任务上与人工标注的一致性达92-94%，WER仅为63%；解码器型LLM的嵌入性能与编码器模型相当；LLM在错误分类中展现出可解释性优势。

Conclusion: LLM为可解释且语义感知的ASR评估提供了有前景的新方向。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) is traditionally evaluated using Word Error Rate (WER), a metric that is insensitive to meaning. Embedding-based semantic metrics are better correlated with human perception, but decoder-based Large Language Models (LLMs) remain underexplored for this task. This paper evaluates their relevance through three approaches: (1) selecting the best hypothesis between two candidates, (2) computing semantic distance using generative embeddings, and (3) qualitative classification of errors. On the HATS dataset, the best LLMs achieve 92--94\% agreement with human annotators for hypothesis selection, compared to 63\% for WER, also outperforming semantic metrics. Embeddings from decoder-based LLMs show performance comparable to encoder models. Finally, LLMs offer a promising direction for interpretable and semantic ASR evaluation.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [AtomicRAG: Atom-Entity Graphs for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20844)
*Yanning Hou,Duanyang Yuan,Sihang Zhou,Xiaoshu Chen,Ke Liang,Siwei Wang,Xinwang Liu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Atom-Entity Graph架构，将知识存储为原子事实（知识原子）而非粗粒度的文本块，结合个性化PageRank与相关性过滤，解决传统GraphRAG中文本块粒度粗、三元组关系抽取易出错的问题。提出的AtomicRAG算法在五个基准数据集上优于强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GraphRAG方法存在两个问题：1) 将文本块作为基本知识单元，刚性组合多个原子事实，缺乏灵活性与适应性；2) 基于三元组的实体链接对关系抽取错误敏感，导致推理路径缺失或错误，影响检索精度。

Method: 提出Atom-Entity Graph架构：知识以"知识原子"（单个、自包含的事实单元）存储，替代粗粒度文本块；实体间边仅表示关系是否存在；结合个性化PageRank与相关性过滤维护准确的实体连接。

Result: 在五个公开基准数据集上，AtomicRAG算法在检索准确性和推理鲁棒性方面优于强RAG基线方法。

Conclusion: 更细粒度的知识原子表示与简化的实体连接方式，能够有效提升GraphRAG的灵活性和检索可靠性。

Abstract: Recent GraphRAG methods integrate graph structures into text indexing and retrieval, using knowledge graph triples to connect text chunks, thereby improving retrieval coverage and precision. However, we observe that treating text chunks as the basic unit of knowledge representation rigidly groups multiple atomic facts together, limiting the flexibility and adaptability needed to support diverse retrieval scenarios. Additionally, triple-based entity linking is sensitive to relation-extraction errors, which can lead to missing or incorrect reasoning paths and ultimately hurt retrieval accuracy. To address these issues, we propose the Atom-Entity Graph, a more precise and reliable architecture for knowledge representation and indexing. In our approach, knowledge is stored as knowledge atoms, namely individual, self-contained units of factual information, rather than coarse-grained text chunks. This allows knowledge elements to be flexibly reassembled without mutual interference, thereby enabling seamless alignment with diverse query perspectives. Edges between entities simply indicate whether a relationship exists. By combining personalized PageRank with relevance-based filtering, we maintain accurate entity connections and improve the reliability of reasoning. Theoretical analysis and experiments on five public benchmarks show that the proposed AtomicRAG algorithm outperforms strong RAG baselines in retrieval accuracy and reasoning robustness. Code: https://github.com/7HHHHH/AtomicRAG.

</details>


### [124] [CaST-POI: Candidate-Conditioned Spatiotemporal Modeling for Next POI Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20845)
*Zhenyu Yu,Chunlei Meng,Yangchen Zeng,Mohd Yamani Idna Idris,Shuigeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CaST-POI提出了一种候选条件感知的下一个兴趣点推荐模型，通过动态地根据候选POI来解读用户历史轨迹，引入候选相关的时空偏置，在三个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常从历史轨迹计算单一用户表示，并统一对所有候选POI打分，但这种候选无关的范式忽略了历史访问的相关性本质上取决于正在评估哪个候选POI。

Method: 提出CaST-POI模型，包含一个候选条件序列读取器，以候选POI作为查询动态关注用户历史；同时引入候选相对时间偏置和候选相对空间偏置，基于历史访问与每个候选POI之间的关系捕捉细粒度移动模式。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，CaST-POI一致性地优于最先进的方法，在多个评估指标上取得了显著改进，在大型候选池条件下优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 候选条件感知的范式能够有效提升下一个POI推荐性能，验证了根据候选POI动态解读用户历史的重要性。

Abstract: Next Point-of-Interest (POI) recommendation plays a crucial role in location-based services by predicting users' future mobility patterns. Existing methods typically compute a single user representation from historical trajectories and use it to score all candidate POIs uniformly. However, this candidate-agnostic paradigm overlooks that the relevance of historical visits inherently depends on which candidate is being evaluated. In this paper, we propose CaST-POI, a candidate-conditioned spatiotemporal model for next POI recommendation. Our key insight is that the same user history should be interpreted differently when evaluating different candidate POIs. CaST-POI employs a candidate-conditioned sequence reader that uses candidates as queries to dynamically attend to user history. In addition, we introduce candidate-relative temporal and spatial biases to capture fine-grained mobility patterns based on the relationships between historical visits and each candidate POI. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that CaST-POI consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, yielding substantial improvements across multiple evaluation metrics, with particularly strong advantages under large candidate pools. Code is available at https://github.com/YuZhenyuLindy/CaST-POI.git.

</details>


### [125] [ADS-POI: Agentic Spatiotemporal State Decomposition for Next Point-of-Interest Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20846)
*Zhenyu Yu,Chunlei Meng,Yangchen Zeng,Mohd Yamani Idna Idris,Shuigeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ADS-POI提出了一种时空状态分解框架，将用户行为分解为多个并行的潜在子状态，每个子状态拥有独立的时空转移动力学，并通过上下文条件机制选择性聚合以预测下一个兴趣点，在三个真实数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将用户历史压缩为单一潜在表示，容易纠缠例行移动模式、短期意图和时间规律等异质信号，限制了状态演化的灵活性和模型适应多样化决策上下文的能力。

Method: ADS-POI用多个并行演化的潜在子状态表示用户，每个子状态由独立的时空转移动力学控制，通过上下文条件机制选择性聚合子状态形成决策状态用于预测，使不同行为组件以不同速率演化并协调一致。

Result: 在Foursquare和Gowalla的三个真实基准数据集上，ADS-POI在全排序评估协议下持续优于强基线方法，分解用户行为为多个时空感知状态带来了更有效和鲁棒的推荐性能。

Conclusion: 将用户行为分解为多个时空感知子状态能够有效提升下一个兴趣点推荐的性能与鲁棒性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Next point-of-interest (POI) recommendation requires modeling user mobility as a spatiotemporal sequence, where different behavioral factors may evolve at different temporal and spatial scales. Most existing methods compress a user's history into a single latent representation, which tends to entangle heterogeneous signals such as routine mobility patterns, short-term intent, and temporal regularities. This entanglement limits the flexibility of state evolution and reduces the model's ability to adapt to diverse decision contexts. We propose ADS-POI, a spatiotemporal state decomposition framework for next POI recommendation. ADS-POI represents a user with multiple parallel evolving latent sub-states, each governed by its own spatiotemporal transition dynamics. These sub-states are selectively aggregated through a context-conditioned mechanism to form the decision state used for prediction. This design enables different behavioral components to evolve at different rates while remaining coordinated under the current spatiotemporal context. Extensive experiments on three real-world benchmark datasets from Foursquare and Gowalla demonstrate that ADS-POI consistently outperforms strong state-of-the-art baselines under a full-ranking evaluation protocol. The results show that decomposing user behavior into multiple spatiotemporally aware states leads to more effective and robust next POI recommendation. Our code is available at https://github.com/YuZhenyuLindy/ADS-POI.git.

</details>


### [126] [Revisiting Content-Based Music Recommendation: Efficient Feature Aggregation from Large-Scale Music Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20847)
*Yizhi Zhou,Jia-Qi Yang,De-Chuan Zhan,Da-Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TASTE，一个整合音频和文本模态的多模态音乐推荐数据集与评测框架，并引入MuQ-token方法高效融合多层音频特征，在候选召回和CTR任务中验证了内容驱动方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音乐推荐系统主要依赖协同过滤，未能充分利用音频信号的内在特征，尤其在冷启动场景下表现不佳。同时，缺乏包含丰富多模态信息（原始音频、文本元数据）的数据集，且现有推荐系统评测框架既未充分利用多模态信息，也不支持多模态算法。

Method: 构建了TASTE数据集，整合音频和文本两种模态；利用大规模自监督音乐编码器提取音频表征；提出MuQ-token方法实现多层音频特征的高效融合。

Result: 在候选召回和CTR任务上，基于音频表征的多模态方法显著优于传统方法；MuQ-token方法在各种设置下均优于其他特征融合技术。

Conclusion: 验证了内容驱动方法在音乐推荐中的有效性，提供了一个高效可复用的多模态基础，为未来研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Music Recommendation Systems (MRSs) are a cornerstone of modern streaming platforms. Existing recommendation models, spanning both recall and ranking stages, predominantly rely on collaborative filtering, which fails to exploit the intrinsic characteristics of audio and consequently leads to suboptimal performance, particularly in cold-start scenarios. However, existing music recommendation datasets often lack rich multimodal information, such as raw audio signals and descriptive textual metadata. Moreover, current recommender system evaluation frameworks remain inadequate, as they neither fully leverage multimodal information nor support a diverse range of algorithms, especially multimodal methods. To address these limitations, we propose TASTE, a comprehensive dataset and benchmarking framework designed to highlight the role of multimodal information in music recommendation. Our dataset integrates both audio and textual modalities. By leveraging recent large-scale self-supervised music encoders, we demonstrate the substantial value of the extracted audio representations across recommendation tasks, including candidate recall and CTR. In addition, we introduce the \textbf{MuQ-token} method, which enables more efficient integration of multi-layer audio features. This method consistently outperforms other feature integration techniques across various settings. Overall, our results not only validate the effectiveness of content-driven approaches but also provide a highly effective and reusable multimodal foundation for future research. Code is available at https://github.com/zreach/TASTE

</details>


### [127] [MATRAG: Multi-Agent Transparent Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Explainable Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20848)
*Sushant Mehta*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MATRAG是一个结合多智能体协作与知识图谱增强检索的可解释推荐框架，通过四个专用智能体（用户建模、物品分析、推理、解释）实现透明推荐，在三个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能，准确率提升12.7%-15.3%，87.4%的生成解释被专家评为有帮助且可信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的推荐系统在可解释性、知识 grounding 和生成连贯解释方面仍面临挑战，现有方法难以建立用户信任。

Method: 提出MATRAG框架，包含四个专用智能体：用户建模智能体构建动态偏好画像，物品分析智能体从知识图谱提取语义特征，推理智能体融合协同过滤与基于内容的信号，解释智能体生成基于检索知识的自然语言解释。引入透明度评分机制量化解释的忠实度与相关性。

Result: 在Amazon Reviews、MovieLens-1M和Yelp三个基准数据集上，推荐准确率提升12.7%（Hit Rate）和15.3%（NDCG）；人工评估中87.4%的生成解释被评为有帮助且可信。

Conclusion: MATRAG为透明、智能体化的推荐系统建立了新基准，并为在生产环境中部署基于LLM的推荐器提供了可操作的见解。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based recommendation systems have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in understanding user preferences and generating personalized suggestions. However, existing approaches face critical challenges in transparency, knowledge grounding, and the ability to provide coherent explanations that foster user trust. We introduce MATRAG (Multi-Agent Transparent Retrieval-Augmented Generation), a novel framework that combined multi-agent collaboration with knowledge graph-augmented retrieval to deliver explainable recommendations. MATRAG employs four specialized agents: a User Modeling Agent that constructs dynamic preference profiles, an Item Analysis Agent that extracts semantic features from knowledge graphs, a Reasoning Agent that synthesizes collaborative and content-based signals, and an Explanation Agent that generates natural language justifications grounded in retrieved knowledge. Our framework incorporates a transparency scoring mechanism that quantifies explanation faithfulness and relevance. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets (Amazon Reviews, MovieLens-1M, and Yelp) demonstrate that MATRAG achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving recommendation accuracy by 12.7\% (Hit Rate) and 15.3\% (NDCG) over leading baselines, while human evaluation confirms that 87.4\% of generated explanations are rated as helpful and trustworthy by domain experts. Our work establishes new benchmarks for transparent, agentic recommendation systems and provides actionable insights for deploying LLM-based recommenders in production environments.

</details>


### [128] [SPIRE: Structure-Preserving Interpretable Retrieval of Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20849)
*Mike Rainey,Umut Acar,Muhammed Sezer*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构感知的检索流水线，通过将文档表示为树结构并定义子文档原语（路径、剪枝、局部/全局上下文化），解决了半结构化HTML文档在检索增强生成中线性分块导致的结构丢失问题。实验表明，该方法在保持可扩展性的同时，能生成更高质量、更多样化的引用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统将半结构化文档（如HTML）线性化为固定大小分块，导致章节结构、列表和表格等结构信息丢失，难以在不损失上下文的前提下返回精确、可引用的证据片段。

Method: 提出结构感知检索流水线：(1) 定义文档原语（路径、路径集、子文档剪枝提取、全局上下文化和局部上下文化两个机制）；(2) 基于句子播种的子文档嵌入候选生成器；(3) 查询时文档感知聚合；(4) 使用局部上下文化视图对检索候选进行重排序的上下文过滤阶段。

Result: 在HTML问答基准上，该方法在固定预算下比强段落基线方法生成更高质量、更多样化的引用，同时保持可扩展性。

Conclusion: 保留文档结构并对选择片段进行上下文化是提升半结构化文档RAG系统性能的关键，可以在上下文物料和精确引证之间取得更好平衡。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation over semi-structured sources such as HTML is constrained by a mismatch between document structure and the flat, sequence-based interfaces of today's embedding and generative models. Retrieval pipelines often linearize documents into fixed-size chunks before indexing, which obscures section structure, lists, and tables, and makes it difficult to return small, citation-ready evidence without losing the surrounding context that makes it interpretable.
  We present a structure-aware retrieval pipeline that operates over tree-structured documents. The core idea is to represent candidates as subdocuments: precise, addressable selections that preserve structural identity while deferring the choice of surrounding context. We define a small set of document primitives--paths and path sets, subdocument extraction by pruning, and two contextualization mechanisms. Global contextualization adds the non-local scaffolding needed to make a selection intelligible (e.g., titles, headers, list and table structure). Local contextualization expands a seed selection within its structural neighborhood to obtain a compact, context-rich view under a target budget. Building on these primitives, we describe an embedding-based candidate generator that indexes sentence-seeded subdocuments and a query-time, document-aware aggregation step that amortizes shared structural context. We then introduce a contextual filtering stage that re-scores retrieved candidates using locally contextualized views.
  Across experiments on HTML question-answering benchmarks, we find that preserving structure while contextualizing selections yields higher-quality, more diverse citations under fixed budgets than strong passage-based baselines, while maintaining scalability.

</details>


### [129] [Association Is Not Similarity: Learning Corpus-Specific Associations for Multi-Hop Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20850)
*Jason Dury*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级的关联增强检索方法（AAR），通过对比学习在嵌入空间中学习段落间的关联关系，在HotpotQA上Recall@5提升8.6个百分点，在困难问题上提升28.5个百分点，但该方法捕获的是语料库特有的共现模式而非可迁移的模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答需要检索具有关联关系的段落，但传统稠密检索仅依赖查询-段落间的语义相似度，无法捕获段落间通过共享推理链形成的关联关系。

Method: 使用小型MLP（4.2M参数）通过对比学习在段落嵌入空间中学习关联关系，推理时对初始稠密检索候选集进行双向关联得分重排序。

Result: HotpotQA上Recall@5从0.831提升至0.916（+8.6），困难问题上提升28.5个百分点；MuSiQue上归纳设置提升10.1个百分点；下游QA任务中EM提升6.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法高效轻量（训练<2分钟，推理增加3.7ms/查询），但学习的是语料库特定的共现模式而非可迁移的关联知识，归纳设置下无显著提升。

Abstract: Dense retrieval systems rank passages by embedding similarity to a query, but multi-hop questions require passages that are associatively related through shared reasoning chains. We introduce Association-Augmented Retrieval (AAR), a lightweight transductive reranking method that trains a small MLP (4.2M parameters) to learn associative relationships between passages in embedding space using contrastive learning on co-occurrence annotations. At inference time, AAR reranks an initial dense retrieval candidate set using bi-directional association scoring. On HotpotQA, AAR improves passage Recall@5 from 0.831 to 0.916 (+8.6 points) without evaluation-set tuning, with gains concentrated on hard questions where the dense baseline fails (+28.5 points). On MuSiQue, AAR achieves +10.1 points in the transductive setting. An inductive model trained on training-split associations and evaluated on unseen validation associations shows no significant improvement, suggesting that the method captures corpus-specific co-occurrences rather than transferable patterns. Ablation studies support this interpretation: training on semantically similar but non-associated passage pairs degrades retrieval below the baseline, while shuffling association pairs causes severe degradation. A downstream QA evaluation shows retrieval gains translate to +6.4 exact match improvement. The method adds 3.7ms per query, trains in under two minutes on a single GPU, and requires no LLM-based indexing.

</details>


### [130] [Robust Test-time Video-Text Retrieval: Benchmarking and Adapting for Query Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20851)
*Bingqing Zhang,Zhuo Cao,Heming Du,Yang Li,Xue Li,Jiajun Liu,Sen Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文发现视频-文本检索模型在查询分布偏移下性能急剧下降，提出了包含12种扰动类型的基准测试，并设计了一种测试时自适应框架HAT-VTR，通过抑制中心性现象和增强时间特征一致性来提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频-文本检索模型在域内基准上表现优异，但面对真实世界中查询数据分布偏移时性能急剧下降，而现有的图像领域鲁棒性方法无法有效处理视频中复杂的时空动态变化。

Method: 首先构建包含12种视频扰动类型、5个严重程度的基准测试；提出HAT-VTR测试时自适应框架，包含Hubness Suppression Memory（用于修正相似度分数）和多粒度损失函数（用于强制时间特征一致性）。

Result: HAT-VTR在多种查询偏移场景下显著优于先前方法，大幅提升了模型的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Conclusion: 通过抑制查询偏移引发的中心性现象，并利用测试时自适应机制，可以有效提升视频-文本检索模型在真实世界应用中的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Modern video-text retrieval (VTR) models excel on in-distribution benchmarks but are highly vulnerable to real-world query shifts, where the distribution of query data deviates from the training domain, leading to a sharp performance drop. Existing image-focused robustness solutions are inadequate to handle this vulnerability in video, as they fail to address the complex spatio-temporal dynamics inherent in these shifts. To systematically evaluate this vulnerability, we first introduce a comprehensive benchmark featuring 12 distinct types of video perturbations across five severity degrees. Analysis on this benchmark reveals that query shifts amplify the hubness phenomenon, where a few gallery items become dominant "hubs" that attract a disproportionate number of queries. To mitigate this, we then propose HAT-VTR (Hubness Alleviation for Test-time Video-Text Retrieval), as our baseline test-time adaptation framework designed to directly counteract hubness in VTR. It leverages two key components: a Hubness Suppression Memory to refine similarity scores, and multi-granular losses to enforce temporal feature consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HAT-VTR substantially improves robustness, consistently outperforming prior methods across diverse query shift scenarios, and enhancing model reliability for real-world applications.

</details>


### [131] [DenoiseRank: Learning to Rank by Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20852)
*Ying Wang,Preslav Nakov,Shangsong Liang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DenoiseRank，一种基于深度生成模型（扩散模型）的学习排序方法，通过加噪和去噪过程预测相关性分布，是首个从生成视角解决传统LTR任务的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LTR模型均基于判别式视角，缺乏从生成式角度建模排序问题的方法。

Method: 提出DenoiseRank模型，在扩散过程中对相关性标签加噪，在反向过程中对查询文档去噪，从而准确预测其分布。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验证明了DenoiseRank的有效性。

Conclusion: DenoiseRank为生成式LTR任务提供了基准方法，开创了从生成式视角解决LTR的先河。

Abstract: Learning to rank (LTR) is one of the core tasks in Machine Learning. Traditional LTR models have made great progress, but nearly all of them are implemented from discriminative perspective. In this paper, we aim at addressing LTR from a novel perspective, i.e., by a deep generative model. Specifically, we propose a novel denoise rank model, DenoiseRank, which noises the relevant labels in the diffusion process and denoises them on the query documents in the reverse process to accurately predict their distribution. Our model is the first to address traditional LTR from generative perspective and is a diffusion method for LTR. Our extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrated the effectiveness of DenoiseRank, and we believe it provides a benchmark for generative LTR task.

</details>


### [132] [A Systematic Study of Biomedical Retrieval Pipeline Trade-offs in Performance and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20853)
*Hayk Stepanyan,Matthew McDermott*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究，系统分析了生物医学文本检索系统中语料库选择、分块粒度、向量索引等管线设计对检索性能与效率的影响，并基于LLM裁判与人工验证的胜率评估，提出了具体的设计建议（如聚合语料库、MedRAG/pubmed作为帕累托最优单语料库、HNSW索引、合适的分块策略及FAISS索引选择）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学与临床自然语言处理中检索系统应用日益广泛，但针对如何构建此类系统的实践指导十分有限，缺乏系统的实证研究来指导研究者做出设计选择。

Method: 在多个公开生物医学文本数据集上，使用多种查询类型（考试题、对话式医学查询、社区提问、非提问形式），系统变化语料库选择、分块粒度、向量索引配置等管线设计，采用LLM作为裁判的胜率对比评估方法，并结合人工验证进行判断。

Result: 语料库聚合在绝对检索质量上表现最优；MedRAG/pubmed是基于图的HNSW索引下的帕累托最优单语料库；发现了合适的分块策略以及FAISS索引选择在速度和效率上的最佳权衡。

Conclusion: 本文为生物医学检索系统构建提供了具体的实践指导，明确了不同管线设计选择对性能和效率的影响，有助于研究者做出更优的设计决策。

Abstract: Retrieval systems are increasingly used in biomedical and clinical natural language processing applications, yet practical guidance for researchers building such systems is limited. In this work, we provide such guidance through an empirical study of how retrieval pipeline design choices affect performance and efficiency at scale.
  In particular, we examine retrieval over a variety of existing, public biomedical text datasets, leveraging a variety of disparate types of queries, including exam-style questions, conversational medical queries, community-asked questions, and non-question formulations across various retrieval pipeline settings spanning corpus selection, chunk granularity, and vector index configuration. Retrieval results are judged using a robust, win-rate comparison assessment via an LLM-as-a-judge setting with human validation.
  Across these experiments, we identify several points of concrete guidance for reviewers, including the superiority of corpus aggregation for absolute retrieval quality, and the emergence of MedRAG/pubmed as the Pareto-optimal singleton corpus under graph-based (HNSW) indexing, appropriate chunking strategies, and FAISS indexing choices that offer the best trade-offs in speed and efficiency.

</details>


### [133] [ERA: Evidence-based Reliability Alignment for Honest Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20854)
*Sunguk Shin,Meeyoung Cha,Byung-Jun Lee,Sungwon Park*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出ERA框架，通过狄利克雷分布建模内外部知识的证据分布，并利用Dempster-Shafer理论量化知识冲突，从而在RAG系统中实现基于证据的可靠性对齐，提升模型在不确定情境下的拒答能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统的可靠性方法仅依赖标量置信度，无法区分认知不确定性（知识缺乏）与偶然不确定性（数据固有歧义），导致在内部参数知识与检索信息发生冲突时，模型无法准确判断何时应拒答。

Method: (1) 上下文证据量化：通过狄利克雷分布将内部知识和外部知识建模为独立的信念质量；(2) 知识冲突量化：利用Dempster-Shafer理论严格度量信息源之间的几何不一致性，从而分离认知不确定性和偶然不确定性，并根据检测到的冲突调整优化目标。

Result: 在标准基准和泛化数据集上，ERA显著优于基线方法，在答案覆盖率与拒答率之间实现了更优的权衡，且具有更好的校准性能。

Conclusion: 将置信度从标量概率转向显式证据分布，并结合DST量化冲突，能有效提升RAG系统在知识冲突场景下的可靠拒答能力。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) grounds language models in factual evidence but introduces critical challenges regarding knowledge conflicts between internalized parameters and retrieved information. However, existing reliability methods, typically relying on scalar confidence, fail to explicitly distinguish between epistemic uncertainty and inherent data ambiguity in such hybrid scenarios. In this paper, we propose a new framework called ERA (Evidence-based Reliability Alignment) to enhance abstention behavior in RAG systems by shifting confidence estimation from scalar probabilities to explicit evidence distributions. Our method consists of two main components: (1) Contextual Evidence Quantification, which models internal and external knowledge as independent belief masses via the Dirichlet distribution, and (2) Quantifying Knowledge Conflict, which leverages Dempster-Shafer Theory (DST) to rigorously measure the geometric discordance between information sources. These components are used to disentangle epistemic uncertainty from aleatoric uncertainty and modulate the optimization objective based on detected conflicts. Experiments on standard benchmarks and a curated generalization dataset demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms baselines, optimizing the trade-off between answer coverage and abstention with superior calibration.

</details>


### [134] [Caesar: Deep Agentic Web Exploration for Creative Answer Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20855)
*Jason Liang,Elliot Meyerson,Risto Miikkulainen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Caesar是一种基于LLM的智能体架构，通过知识图谱进行联想推理，并采用对抗性草稿精炼循环生成新颖、结构连贯的创意内容，显著优于现有研究型智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体框架倾向于聚合搜索，生成的是衍生性摘要而非创造性发现；自主智能体需要从被动检索转向主动、深层的联想综合，以发现新思想。

Method: Caesar由两个组件构成：(1) 基于动态上下文感知策略的探索，利用知识图谱促进联想推理；(2) 由对抗性草稿精炼循环控制的合成，主动寻求新颖视角而非确认已有先验。

Result: Caesar生成的人工制品和答案具有高新颖性和结构连贯性，在需要创造力的任务中显著超越了最先进的LLM研究型智能体。

Conclusion: 通过知识图谱驱动的联想推理与对抗性精炼相结合，Caesar弥合了信息收集与新颖见解合成之间的鸿沟，实现了从被动检索到创造性发现的范式跃迁。

Abstract: To advance from passive retrieval to creative discovery of new ideas, autonomous agents must be capable of deep, associative synthesis. However, current agentic frameworks prioritize convergent search, often resulting in derivative summaries that lack creativity. Caesar is an agentic LLM architecture designed to bridge the gap between information gathering and synthesis of new insights. Unlike existing agents that treat the web as a flat sequence of disconnected documents, Caesar leverages an extensive knowledge graph to foster associative reasoning, thus enabling the discovery of non-obvious connections between disparate concepts. It consists of two components: (1) exploration driven by a dynamic context-aware policy, and (2) synthesis controlled by an adversarial draft refinement loop that actively seeks novel perspectives rather than confirming established priors. Caesar demonstrates the ability to generate artifacts and answers characterized by high novelty and structural coherence, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art LLM research agents in tasks requiring creativity.

</details>


### [135] [DiagramBank: A Large-scale Dataset of Diagram Design Exemplars with Paper Metadata for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20857)
*Tingwen Zhang,Ling Yue,Zhen Xu,Shaowu Pan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DiagramBank是一个包含89,422张来自顶级科学出版物的示意图数据集，旨在通过检索增强生成技术解决AI科学家系统中出版级科学图表自动生成的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科学家系统在自动生成论文文本和代码方面取得进展，但生成出版级科学图表（如teaser图）仍是端到端论文生成的主要瓶颈。teaser图作为战略性的视觉界面，需要概念综合和规划，而现有系统要么忽略这一组件，要么使用低质量替代方案。

Method: 通过自动化策展流程收集高质量图表，包括：从顶级出版物中提取图表及文中引用；使用CLIP基过滤器区分示意图与标准数据图或自然图像；为每个实例配备从摘要、标题到图表-引用对的丰富上下文信息，支持多粒度查询下的信息检索。

Result: 构建了包含89,422张示意图的大规模数据集DiagramBank，提供了即用索引格式，并发布了检索增强生成代码库，用于基于示例条件的teaser图合成。

Conclusion: DiagramBank填补了AI科学家系统中高质量示意图生成的空白，通过大规模数据集和检索增强生成方法，为自动化科学图表合成提供了重要基础资源。

Abstract: Recent advances in autonomous ``AI scientist'' systems have demonstrated the ability to automatically write scientific manuscripts and codes with execution. However, producing a publication-grade scientific diagram (e.g., teaser figure) is still a major bottleneck in the ``end-to-end'' paper generation process. For example, a teaser figure acts as a strategic visual interface and serves a different purpose than derivative data plots. It demands conceptual synthesis and planning to translate complex logic workflow into a compelling graphic that guides intuition and sparks curiosity. Existing AI scientist systems usually omit this component or fall back to an inferior alternative. To bridge this gap, we present DiagramBank, a large-scale dataset consisting of 89,422 schematic diagrams curated from existing top-tier scientific publications, designed for multimodal retrieval and exemplar-driven scientific figure generation. DiagramBank is developed through our automated curation pipeline that extracts figures and corresponding in-text references, and uses a CLIP-based filter to differentiate schematic diagrams from standard plots or natural images. Each instance is paired with rich context from abstract, caption, to figure-reference pairs, enabling information retrieval under different query granularities. We release DiagramBank in a ready-to-index format and provide a retrieval-augmented generation codebase to demonstrate exemplar-conditioned synthesis of teaser figures. DiagramBank is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/zhangt20/DiagramBank with code at https://github.com/csml-rpi/DiagramBank.

</details>


### [136] [Mixture of Sequence: Theme-Aware Mixture-of-Experts for Long-Sequence Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20858)
*Xiao Lin,Zhicheng Tang,Weilin Cong,Mengyue Hang,Kai Wang,Yajuan Wang,Zhichen Zeng,Ting-Wei Li,Hyunsik Yoo,Zhining Liu,Xuying Ning,Ruizhong Qiu,Wen-yen Chen,Shuo Chang,Rong Jin,Huayu Li,Hanghang Tong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MoS框架，一种模型无关的混合专家方法，通过主题感知路由机制和多尺度融合机制，从长序列中提取主题特定和多尺度的子序列，有效解决用户兴趣漂移导致的序列推荐问题，在减少计算量的同时取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列推荐中长序列建模面临用户兴趣显著漂移带来的无关或误导信息挑战，实证分析发现用户存在"session hopping"行为模式（兴趣在短时间窗口内稳定，跨session剧烈变化且可能重现）。

Method: 提出MoS框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 主题感知路由机制，自适应学习用户序列的潜在主题并将序列组织成多个连贯子序列，每个子序列只包含与特定主题对齐的session；(2) 多尺度融合机制，利用三类专家（全局序列特征、短期用户行为、主题特定语义模式）捕获多角度多尺度信息。

Result: MoS在多个数据集上一致达到SOTA性能，同时相比其他MoE方法引入更少的FLOPs，在效用和效率之间取得了优秀的平衡。

Conclusion: MoS通过主题感知路由有效过滤session hopping带来的噪声信息，多尺度融合避免信息损失，为长序列推荐提供了一种高效且准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation has rapidly advanced in click-through rate prediction due to its ability to model dynamic user interests. A key challenge, however, lies in modeling long sequences: users often exhibit significant interest shifts, introducing substantial irrelevant or misleading information.
