Our empirical analysis corroborates this challenge and uncovers a recurring behavioral pattern in long sequences (\textit{session hopping}): user interests remain stable within short temporal spans (\textit{sessions}) but shift drastically across sessions and may reappear after multiple sessions. To address this challenge, we propose the Mixture of Sequence (MoS) framework, a model-agnostic MoE approach that achieves accurate predictions by extracting theme-specific and multi-scale subsequences from noisy raw user sequences. First, MoS employs a theme-aware routing mechanism to adaptively learn the latent themes of user sequences and organizes these sequences into multiple coherent subsequences. Each subsequence contains only sessions aligned with a specific theme, thereby effectively filtering out irrelevant or even misleading information introduced by user interest shifts in session hopping. In addition, to alleviate potential information loss, we introduce a multi-scale fusion mechanism, which leverages three types of experts to capture global sequence characteristics, short-term user behaviors, and theme-specific semantic patterns. Together, these two mechanisms endow MoS with the ability to deliver accurate recommendations from multi-faceted and multi-scale perspectives. Experimental results demonstrate that MoS consistently achieves the SOTA performance while introducing fewer FLOPs compared with other MoE counterparts, providing strong evidence of its excellent balance between utility and efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/xiaolin-cs/MoS.

</details>


### [137] [KGiRAG: An Iterative GraphRAG Approach for Responding Sensemaking Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20859)
*Isabela Iacob,Melisa Marian,Gheorghe Cosmin Silaghi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种迭代式、基于反馈的GraphRAG架构，通过响应质量评估不断迭代优化输出，相比单次基线方法在HotPotQA数据集上生成语义质量和相关性更高的回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在RAG管线中存在幻觉问题，且处理复杂查询时上下文长度存在技术限制，难以充分支撑查询所需的 grounding。

Method: 提出一种迭代式、基于反馈的GraphRAG架构，利用响应质量评估对输出进行反复迭代优化，直到生成合理且充分 grounded 的回答。

Result: 在HotPotQA数据集上的评估表明，该迭代RAG策略相比单次基线方法，生成的回答具有更高的语义质量和更好的相关性。

Conclusion: 迭代式的反馈驱动GraphRAG能有效缓解LLM幻觉和上下文长度限制问题，提升复杂查询的回答质量。

Abstract: Recent literature highlights the potential of graph-based approaches within large language model (LLM) retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipelines for answering queries of varying complexity, particularly those that fall outside the LLM's prior knowledge. However, LLMs are prone to hallucination and often face technical limitations in handling contexts large enough to ground complex queries effectively. To address these challenges, we propose a novel iterative, feedback-driven GraphRAG architecture that leverages response quality assessment to iteratively refine outputs until a sound, well-grounded response is produced. Evaluating our approach with queries from the HotPotQA dataset, we demonstrate that this iterative RAG strategy yields responses with higher semantic quality and improved relevance compared to a single-shot baseline.

</details>


### [138] [RealRoute: Dynamic Query Routing System via Retrieve-then-Verify Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20860)
*Jiahe Liu,Qinkai Yu,Jingcheng Niu,Xi Zhu,Zirui He,Zhen Xiang,Fan Yang,Jinman Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RealRoute通过"检索-验证"机制替代传统的"LLM作为路由"策略，实现多源异构知识的鲁棒检索与事实性回答生成，在多头推理任务中显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法采用LLM作为路由器预测性地将子查询分发到特定数据源，但这类"预测性路由"策略严重依赖数据源的语义边界，当源边界模糊时容易产生路由错误，导致信息遗漏或错误。

Method: 提出RealRoute系统，将范式从预测性路由转向鲁棒的检索-验证机制：1) 并行、源无关的检索确保证据完整性；2) 动态验证器交叉核验检索结果并综合生成基于事实的回答。

Result: 在多跳RAG推理任务中，RealRoute显著优于预测性基线方法。系统以开源工具包形式发布，提供用户友好的Web界面和实时"重新路由"过程可视化。

Conclusion: RealRoute证明了从预测性路由到检索-验证的范式转换能有效解决异构数据源场景下的路由错误问题，为多源RAG提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Despite the success of Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) in grounding LLMs with external knowledge, its application over heterogeneous sources (e.g., private databases, global corpora, and APIs) remains a significant challenge. Existing approaches typically employ an LLM-as-a-Router to dispatch decomposed sub-queries to specific sources in a predictive manner. However, this "LLM-as-a-Router" strategy relies heavily on the semantic meaning of different data sources, often leading to routing errors when source boundaries are ambiguous. In this work, we introduce RealRoute System, a framework that shifts the paradigm from predictive routing to a robust Retrieve-then-Verify mechanism. RealRoute ensures \textit{evidence completeness through parallel, source-agnostic retrieval, followed by a dynamic verifier that cross-checks the results and synthesizes a factually grounded answer}. Our demonstration allows users to visualize the real-time "re-routing" process and inspect the verification chain across multiple knowledge silos. Experiments show that RealRoute significantly outperforms predictive baselines in the multi-hop Rag reasoning task. The RealRoute system is released as an open-source toolkit with a user-friendly web interface. The code is available at the URL: https://github.com/Joseph1951210/RealRoute.

</details>


### [139] [Deep Interest Mining with Cross-Modal Alignment for SemanticID Generation in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20861)
*Yagchen Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种生成式推荐框架，通过深度上下文兴趣挖掘、跨模态语义对齐和质量感知强化机制，解决现有语义ID生成中信息退化、语义退化和模态扭曲三大问题，在多个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义ID生成方法存在三个关键缺陷：(1)两阶段压缩导致信息退化，缺乏后验机制区分SID质量；(2)级联量化丢弃原始多模态特征中的关键语义信息，嵌入生成与量化阶段未联合优化；(3)量化器无法正确对齐文本和图像模态，导致特征错位。

Method: 提出三个核心创新模块：(1)利用视觉语言模型将非文本模态对齐到统一文本语义空间，缓解模态扭曲；(2)深度上下文兴趣挖掘，通过重建监督捕获广告上下文中隐含的高层语义信息；(3)质量感知强化学习框架，在后验阶段鼓励语义丰富的SID并抑制低质量SID。

Result: 在多个基准上持续超越现有最优SID生成方法，消融实验验证了每个组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化多模态对齐、深度语义挖掘和后验质量评估，显著提升了生成式推荐中语义ID的信息保真度和推荐性能。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GR) has demonstrated remarkable performance in next-token prediction paradigms, which relies on Semantic IDs (SIDs) to compress trillion-scale data into learnable vocabulary sequences. However, existing methods suffer from three critical limitations: (1) Information Degradation: the two-stage compression pipeline causes semantic loss and information degradation, with no posterior mechanism to distinguish high-quality from low-quality SIDs; (2) Semantic Degradation: cascaded quantization discards key semantic information from original multimodal features, as the embedding generation and quantization stages are not jointly optimized toward a unified objective; (3) Modality Distortion: quantizers fail to properly align text and image modalities, causing feature misalignment even when upstream networks have aligned them. To address these challenges, we propose a novel framework integrating three key innovations: Deep Contextual Interest Mining (DCIM), Cross-Modal Semantic Alignment (CMSA), and Quality-Aware Reinforcement Mechanism (QARM). First, we leverage Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to align non-textual modalities into a unified text-based semantic space, mitigating modality distortion. Second, we introduce a deep interest mining mechanism that captures high-level semantic information implicitly present in advertising contexts, encouraging SIDs to preserve critical contextual information through reconstruction-based supervision. Third, we employ a reinforcement learning framework with quality-aware rewards to encourage semantically rich SIDs while suppressing low-quality ones in the posterior stage. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art SID generation methods, achieving superior performance on multiple benchmarks. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of each proposed component

</details>


### [140] [Following the Eye-Tracking Evidence: Established Web-Search Assumptions Fail in Carousel Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21019)
*Jingwei Kang,Maarten de Rijke,Harrie Oosterhuis*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过眼动实验数据，检验了轮播界面中用户行为的三个传统假设（F型模式、检验假设、标题关注），发现F型模式仅在垂直浏览中成立而非水平滑动，检验假设不成立，且用户忽略标题直接关注内容，表明搜索界面的行为假设不适用于轮播推荐界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮播界面作为流媒体服务的事实标准已超过十年，但用户行为研究极为匮乏。由于缺乏实证研究，先前工作假设单列表搜索界面中的行为模式（如F型模式、检验假设）同样适用于轮播界面，这些假设被用于设计点击模型和评价指标。本文旨在通过眼动追踪数据验证这些假设是否成立。

Method: 分析近期发布的轮播界面眼动追踪实验数据集，从三个方面检验用户行为假设：(i) F型模式在垂直浏览与水平滑动中的表现；(ii) 基于浏览的点击率（examination-conditioned CTR）验证检验假设；(iii) 用户对轮播标题的关注程度。

Result: (i) F型模式仅适用于垂直浏览，不适用于水平滑动；条件于点击时，用户浏览呈现轮播界面特有的L型模式。(ii) 基于浏览的点击率表明检验假设在轮播界面中不成立。(iii) 与先前假设相反，用户通常忽略轮播标题，直接关注内容项。

Conclusion: 许多用户行为假设（尤其是浏览模式）无法从搜索界面迁移至轮播推荐界面。该领域缺乏构建用户行为模型的可靠基础，因此有必要重新评估现有轮播界面的评价指标和点击模型。

Abstract: Carousel interfaces have been the de-facto standard for streaming media services for over a decade. Yet, there has been very little research into user behavior with such interfaces, which thus remains poorly understood. Due to this lack of empirical research, previous work has assumed that behaviors established in single-list web-search interfaces, such as the F-pattern and the examination hypothesis, also apply to carousel interfaces, for instance when designing click models or evaluation metrics. We analyze a recently-released interaction and examination dataset resulting from an eye-tracking study performed on carousel interfaces to verify whether these assumptions actually hold.
  We find that (i)~the F-pattern holds only for vertical examination and not for horizontal swiping; additionally, we discover that, when conditioned on a click, user examination follows an L-pattern unique to carousel interfaces; (ii)~click-through-rates conditioned on examination indicate that the well-known examination hypothesis does not hold in carousel interfaces; and (iii)~contrary to the assumptions of previous work, users generally ignore carousel headings and focus directly on the content items. Our findings show that many user behavior assumptions, especially concerning examination patterns, do not transfer from web search interfaces to carousel recommendation settings. Our work shows that the field lacks a reliable foundation on which to build models of user behavior with these interfaces. Consequently, a re-evaluation of existing metrics and click models for carousel interfaces may be warranted.

</details>


### [141] [Automated Extraction of Pharmacokinetic Parameters from Structured XML Scientific Articles: Enhancing Data Accessibility at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21063)
*Remya Ampadi Ramachandran,Lisa A. Tell,Sidharth Rai,Nuwan Millagaha Gedara,Hossein Sholehrasa,Jim E. Riviere,Majid Jaberi-Douraki*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 药理学领域缺乏集中、全面且最新的药代动力学（PK）数据存储库，现有PK数据主要以表格形式分散于各类出版物中。FARAD组织40多年来一直手动提取这些表格数据，但面对日益增长的文献量和人力短缺，迫切需要开发AI算法来自动检测和提取表格数据，以解决手动提取耗时、易错的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药理学领域缺乏集中、全面的PK数据存储库，从分散的科学出版物中收集定量PK参数耗时且困难；同时，手动提取表格数据工作量大、易出错，加之人力短缺，迫切需要自动化解决方案。

Method: 开发能够精准处理表格结构的AI算法，用于表格检测和数据提取，要求算法能根据列和/或行头信息正确解析单元格组织方式，模拟人类读者的自然理解方式。

Result: 尚未在摘要中呈现具体实验结果，仅明确了需要开发AI算法以实现自动化表格数据提取的目标。

Conclusion: 由于手动提取表格数据日益不可行，必须开发AI算法来实现PK数据表格的自动检测与提取，以应对文献量激增和人力短缺的挑战。

Abstract: In the field of pharmacology, there is a notable absence of centralized, comprehensive, and up-to-date repositories of PK data. This poses a significant challenge for R&D as it can be a time-consuming and challenging task to collect all the required quantitative PK parameters from diverse scientific publications. This quantitative PK information is predominantly organized in tabular format, mostly available as XML, HTML, or PDF files within various online repositories and scientific publications, including supplementary materials. This makes tables one of the crucial components and information elements of scientific or regulatory documents as they are commonly utilized to present quantitative information. Extracting data from tables is typically a labor-intensive process, and alternative automated machine learning models may struggle to accurately detect and extract the relevant data due to the complex nature and diverse layouts of tabular data. The difficulty of information extraction and reading order detection is largely dependent on the structural complexity of the tables. Efforts to understand tables should prioritize capturing the content of table cells in a manner that aligns with how a human reader naturally comprehends the information. FARAD has been manually extracting tabular data and other information from literature and regulatory agencies for over 40 years. However, there is now an urgent need to automate this process due to the large volume of publications released daily. The accuracy of this task has become increasingly challenging, as manual extraction is tedious and prone to errors, especially given the staffing shortages we are currently facing. This necessitates the development of AI algorithms for table detection and extraction that are able to precisely handle cells organized according to the table structure, as indicated by column and/or row header information.

</details>


### [142] [Multilingual and Domain-Agnostic Tip-of-the-Tongue Query Generation for Simulated Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21096)
*Xuhong He,To Eun Kim,Maik Fröbe,Jaime Arguello,Bhaskar Mitra,Fernando Diaz*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个大规模多语言Tip-of-the-Tongue (ToT)检索基准，涵盖中文、日语、韩语和英语，通过基于LLM的查询模拟框架生成每语言5000条查询，并验证了语言感知的查询模拟设计对保真度的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ToT检索基准主要集中在英语，缺乏多语言场景下的评估资源，限制了多语言信息检索的研究与应用。

Method: 采用基于LLM的查询模拟框架构建多语言ToT测试集；系统研究提示语言和源文档语言对模拟查询保真度的影响；通过系统排序相关性分析验证合成查询的有效性。

Result: 生成了四个语言（中、日、韩、英）各5000条查询的多领域ToT测试集；发现非英语源语言普遍重要，而英语维基百科在非英语源信息不足时有助于查询生成。

Conclusion: 有效的ToT模拟需要语言感知的设计策略，该工作为英语之外的现实ToT数据集构建提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Tip-of-the-Tongue (ToT) retrieval benchmarks have largely focused on English, limiting their applicability to multilingual information access. In this work, we construct multilingual ToT test collections for Chinese, Japanese, Korean, and English, using an LLM-based query simulation framework. We systematically study how prompt language and source document language affect the fidelity of simulated ToT queries, validating synthetic queries through system rank correlation against real user queries. Our results show that effective ToT simulation requires language-aware design choices: non-English language sources are generally important, while English Wikipedia can be beneficial when non-English sources provide insufficient information for query generation. Based on these findings, we release four ToT test collections with 5,000 queries per language across multiple domains. This work provides the first large-scale multilingual ToT benchmark and offers practical guidance for constructing realistic ToT datasets beyond English.

</details>


### [143] [PAPERMIND: Benchmarking Agentic Reasoning and Critique over Scientific Papers in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21304)
*Yanjun Zhao,Tianxin Wei,Jiaru Zou,Xuying Ning,Yuanchen Bei,Lingjie Chen,Simmi Rana,Wendy H. Yang,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PAPERMIND是一个评估多模态大模型对科学论文进行整合推理能力的基准，涵盖多模态对齐、实验解释、跨源证据推理和批判性评估四个任务家族，实验发现现有模型在整合科学推理方面存在持续的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常孤立地评估科学论文理解的不同能力（如问答、摘要），缺乏一个统一框架来评估这些能力作为相互关联的认知过程的表现，难以全面衡量模型对科学论文的整合推理能力。

Method: 从农业、生物、化学、计算机科学、医学、物理和经济学七个领域的真实科学论文构建PAPERMIND基准，设计四个互补的任务家族（多模态对齐、实验解释、跨源证据推理、批判性评估），分别对应科学论文推理的不同认知维度，通过多任务行为分析实现诊断性评估。

Result: 在多种开源和闭源多模态大模型上的实验表明，不同任务之间存在一致性的性能差距，揭示了模型在整合科学推理和批判性评估方面存在持续的挑战。

Conclusion: PAPERMIND为评估和诊断多模态大模型的整合科学推理能力提供了一个系统化的基准，揭示了现有模型在综合理解科学论文方面的显著局限性。

Abstract: Understanding scientific papers requires more than answering isolated questions or summarizing content. It involves an integrated reasoning process that grounds textual and visual information, interprets experimental evidence, synthesizes information across sources, and critically evaluates scientific claims. However, existing benchmarks typically assess these abilities in isolation, making it difficult to evaluate scientific paper understanding as a unified set of interacting cognitive abilities. In this work, we introduce PAPERMIND, a benchmark designed to evaluate integrated and agent-oriented scientific reasoning over research papers. PAPERMIND is constructed from real scientific papers across seven domains, including agriculture, biology, chemistry, computer science, medicine, physics, and economics. It comprises four complementary task families that collectively operationalize distinct cognitive facets of scientific paper reasoning, including multimodal grounding, experimental interpretation, cross-source evidence reasoning, and critical assessment. By analyzing model behavior across multiple tasks, PAPERMIND enables a diagnostic evaluation of integrated scientific reasoning behaviors that are difficult to assess through isolated task evaluations. Extensive experiments on both opensource and closed-source multimodal LLMs reveal consistent performance gaps across tasks, highlighting persistent challenges in integrated scientific reasoning and critique. Our benchmark and dataset are available at https:// github.com/Yanjun-Zhao/PaperMind.

</details>


### [144] [WPGRec: Wavelet Packet Guided Graph Enhanced Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21305)
*Peilin Liu,Zhiquan Ji,Gang Yan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: WPGRec提出了一种统一时频图增强框架，通过无抽取平稳小波包变换实现多尺度子带分解，并在对应尺度上进行子带级图传播以注入协同信息，最后通过能量与频谱平坦度感知的门控融合模块自适应聚合子带，在四个公开数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有频域方法中，全局谱操作易混淆局部瞬态与长程依赖，滤波器式小波方法存在时间错位与边界伪影；同时，协同信号通常通过尺度不一致的辅助模块注入，限制了时域动态与结构依赖联合建模的收益。

Method: 1）采用全树无抽取平稳小波包变换生成等长、平移不变子带序列；2）在子带级别进行交互图传播，注入高阶协同信息同时保持跨分辨率的时间对齐；3）设计能量与频谱平坦度感知的门控融合模块，自适应聚合信息子带并抑制噪声成分。

Result: 在四个公开基准数据集上，WPGRec一致优于序列推荐和图增强基线，在稀疏和行为复杂的数据集上提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 带一致性结构注入与自适应子带融合对序列推荐任务有效，验证了多分辨率时域建模与图传播在匹配尺度上对齐的必要性。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation aims to model users' evolving interests from noisy and non-stationary interaction streams, where long-term preferences, short-term intents, and localized behavioral fluctuations may coexist across temporal scales. Existing frequency-domain methods mainly rely on either global spectral operations or filter-based wavelet processing. However, global spectral operations tend to entangle local transients with long-range dependencies, while filter-based wavelet pipelines may suffer from temporal misalignment and boundary artifacts during multi-scale decomposition and reconstruction. Moreover, collaborative signals from the user-item interaction graph are often injected through scale-inconsistent auxiliary modules, limiting the benefit of jointly modeling temporal dynamics and structural dependencies. To address these issues, we propose Wavelet Packet Guided Graph Enhanced Sequential Recommendation (WPGRec), a unified time-frequency and graph-enhanced framework that aligns multi-resolution temporal modeling with graph propagation at matching scales. WPGRec first applies a full-tree undecimated stationary wavelet packet transform to generate equal-length, shift-invariant subband sequences. It then performs subband-wise interaction-graph propagation to inject high-order collaborative information while preserving temporal alignment across resolutions. Finally, an energy- and spectral-flatness-aware gated fusion module adaptively aggregates informative subbands and suppresses noise-like components. Extensive experiments on four public benchmarks show that WPGRec consistently outperforms sequential and graph-based baselines, with particularly clear gains on sparse and behaviorally complex datasets, highlighting the effectiveness of band-consistent structure injection and adaptive subband fusion for sequential recommendation.

</details>


### [145] [From Tokens to Concepts: Leveraging SAE for SPLADE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21511)
*Yuxuan Zong,Mathias Vast,Basile Van Cooten,Laure Soulier,Benjamin Piwowarski*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SAE-SPLADE模型，用稀疏自编码器（SAE）学习潜在语义概念空间替代传统SPLADE的骨干词汇表，解决了多义词、同义词问题，并在多语言和多模态场景中具有潜力。实验表明，SAE-SPLADE在领域内和跨领域检索任务中性能与SPLADE相当，同时效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学习型稀疏IR模型（如SPLADE）依赖骨干词汇表，存在多义性和同义词问题，且在多语言和多模态场景中面临挑战。作者希望通过引入稀疏自编码器学习语义概念空间来替代词汇表，从而克服这些局限。

Method: 用稀疏自编码器（SAE）学习潜在语义概念空间，替代SPLADE原有的骨干词汇表。研究了SAE与SPLADE两种概念的兼容性，探索了不同的训练策略，并对所提出的SAE-SPLADE与传统SPLADE进行了对比分析。

Result: SAE-SPLADE在领域内（in-domain）和跨领域（out-of-domain）检索任务中取得了与SPLADE相当的检索性能，同时效率有所提升。

Conclusion: 用SAE学习语义概念空间替代词汇表是可行的，能够保持检索性能的同时提升效率，并为解决多语言、多模态场景下的稀疏检索问题提供了新方向。

Abstract: Learned Sparse IR models, such as SPLADE, offer an excellent efficiency-effectiveness tradeoff. However, they rely on the underlying backbone vocabulary, which might hinder performance (polysemicity and synonymy) and pose a challenge for multi-lingual and multi-modal usages. To solve this limitation, we propose to replace the backbone vocabulary with a latent space of semantic concepts learned using Sparse Auto-Encoders (SAE). Throughout this paper, we study the compatibility of these 2 concepts, explore training approaches, and analyze the differences between our SAE-SPLADE model and traditional SPLADE models. Our experiments demonstrate that SAE-SPLADE achieves retrieval performance comparable to SPLADE on both in-domain and out-of-domain tasks while offering improved efficiency.

</details>


### [146] [Pre-trained LLMs Meet Sequential Recommenders: Efficient User-Centric Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21536)
*Nikita Severin,Danil Kartushov,Vladislav Urzhumov,Vladislav Kulikov,Oksana Konovalova,Alexey Grishanov,Anton Klenitskiy,Artem Fatkulin,Alexey Vasilev,Andrey Savchenko,Ilya Makarov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种知识蒸馏方法，将预训练LLM生成的文本用户画像注入序列推荐系统，在保持传统模型推理效率的同时提升用户语义理解，无需架构修改或LLM微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐系统局限于交互模式建模，缺乏对丰富用户语义的捕捉；LLM虽有推理能力但实时推理成本过高，无法直接部署。

Method: 利用预训练LLM生成文本用户画像，通过知识蒸馏将画像信息注入序列推荐模型，推理阶段无需LLM参与。

Result: 在保持传统序列模型推理效率的前提下提升了推荐性能，无需修改模型架构或对LLM进行微调。

Conclusion: 该方法在避免LLM实时推理开销的同时，有效提升了序列推荐系统的用户语义理解能力，具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Sequential recommender systems have achieved significant success in modeling temporal user behavior but remain limited in capturing rich user semantics beyond interaction patterns. Large Language Models (LLMs) present opportunities to enhance user understanding with their reasoning capabilities, yet existing integration approaches create prohibitive inference costs in real time. To address these limitations, we present a novel knowledge distillation method that utilizes textual user profile generated by pre-trained LLMs into sequential recommenders without requiring LLM inference at serving time. The resulting approach maintains the inference efficiency of traditional sequential models while requiring neither architectural modifications nor LLM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [147] [Counterfactual Multi-task Learning for Delayed Conversion Modeling in E-commerce Sales Pre-Promotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21675)
*Xin Song,Kaiyuan Li,Jinxin Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对促销活动前用户延迟转化（加购后促销日下单）导致的CVR预测偏差问题，提出CM-DCM模型，通过多任务学习、个性化门控和反事实因果建模，有效提升了促销前场景的CVR预测效果，并在线上A/B测试中显著提升了广告收入和GMV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CVR预测研究主要关注促销日活动数据，忽略了促销前关键时期；同时，延迟转化建模未考虑促销前场景中转化分布的特殊偏移（延迟转化主要集中在促销日而非连续时间窗口）。

Method: 提出CM-DCM模型，包含三个核心创新：(i) 多任务架构，利用促销前历史数据联合建模直接转化与延迟转化；(ii) 个性化用户行为门控模块，缓解促销前短时间内的数据稀疏问题；(iii) 反事实因果方法，建模从加购(ATC)到延迟转化的转移概率。

Result: 离线实验表明CM-DCM在促销前场景中优于基线模型；线上A/B测试在大型促销活动中显著提升了广告收入、延迟转化GMV和整体GMV。

Conclusion: CM-DCM有效解决了促销前延迟转化预测问题，验证了反因果多任务建模在促销场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Sales promotions, as short-term incentives to stimulate product purchases, play a pivotal role in modern e-commerce marketing strategies. During promotional events, user behavior patterns exhibit distinct characteristics compared to regular periods. In the pre-promotion phase, users typically engage in product search and browsing without immediate purchases, adding items to carts in anticipation of promotional discounts. This behavior leads to delayed conversions, resulting in significantly lower conversion rates (CVR) before the promotion day. Although existing research has made progress in CVR prediction for promotion days using historical data, it largely overlooks the critical pre-promotion period. And delayed feedback modeling has been extensively studied, current approaches fail to account for the unique distribution shifts in conversion behavior before promotional events, where delayed conversions predominantly occur on the promotion day rather than over continuous time windows. To address these limitations, we propose the Counterfactual Multi-task Delayed Conversion Model (CM-DCM), which leverages historical pre-promotion data to enhance CVR prediction for both delayed and direct conversions. Our model incorporates three key innovations: (i) A multi-task architecture that jointly models direct and delayed conversions using historical pre-promotion data; (ii) A personalized user behavior gating module to mitigate data sparsity issues during brief pre-promotion periods; (iii) A counterfactual causal approach to model the transition probability from add-to-cart (ATC) to delayed conversion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CM-DCM outperforms baselines in pre-promotion scenarios. Online A/B tests during major promotional events showed significant improvements in advertising revenue, delayed conversion GMV, and overall GMV, validating the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [148] [Multistakeholder Impacts of Profile Portability in a Recommender Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21750)
*Anas Buhayh,Elizabeth McKinnie,Clement Canel,Robin Burke*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了推荐系统中算法多元主义（中间件）与数据可移植性政策对用户模型和利益相关者结果的影响，发现不同数据可移植性场景对不同推荐算法的用户效用产生差异化影响，并提出了设计公平推荐生态系统的政策建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统的优化传统上聚焦于算法层面的干预（如多目标模型、重排序），而忽略了推荐生态系统本身的结构性变化。算法多元主义（用户可自主选择推荐算法）虽然已被证明有利于小众消费者和提供商，但用户切换算法时的数据可移植性问题尚未被充分研究。本文旨在探讨数据可移植性政策如何影响用户模型及各利益相关者的结果。

Method: 通过分析数据可移植性政策（如用户数据所有权和控制权相关法规）在推荐系统中的应用，研究不同数据可移植性场景下用户效用如何随推荐算法变化。

Result: 数据可移植性场景对用户效用产生差异化影响，具体效果取决于所使用的推荐算法类型。

Conclusion: 提出了设计公平推荐生态系统的关键政策考量与启示，强调数据可移植性政策在算法多元主义背景下对利益相关者结果的重要调节作用。

Abstract: Optimizing outcomes for multiple stakeholders in recommender systems has historically focused on algorithmic interventions, such as developing multi-objective models or re-ranking results from existing algorithms. However, structural changes to the recommendation ecosystem itself remain understudied. This paper explores the implications of algorithmic pluralism (also known as "middleware" in the governance literature), in which recommendation algorithms are decoupled from platforms, enabling users to select their preferred algorithm. Prior simulation work demonstrates that algorithmic choice benefits niche consumers and providers. Yet this approach raises critical questions about user modeling in the context of data portability: when users switch algorithms, what happens to their data? Noting that multiple data portability regulations have emerged to strengthen user data ownership and control. We examine how such policies affect user models and stakeholders' outcomes in recommendation setting. Our findings reveal that data portability scenarios produce varying effects on user utility across different recommendation algorithms. We highlight key policy considerations and implications for designing equitable recommendation ecosystems.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Frequency-Forcing: From Scaling-as-Time to Soft Frequency Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20902)
*Weitao Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Frequency-Forcing，通过轻量级可学习小波包变换生成的自引导信号，以软约束方式实现频率排序的流匹配生成，在ImageNet-256上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流匹配模型均匀地将噪声传输到数据，而引入显式生成顺序（先粗粒度低频结构，后细粒度细节）对自然图像合成非常有效。现有K-Flow和Latent Forcing分别以硬约束和软约束方式实现这一目标，但前者需重写流坐标，后者依赖重型预训练编码器。作者希望结合二者优势，实现一种无需外部依赖、路径保持的频率排序生成机制。

Method: 提出Frequency-Forcing方法：采用标准像素流作为主生成流，同时通过轻量级可学习小波包变换从数据本身提取低频辅助流，该辅助流在时间上更早成熟，以软约束方式引导主流的生成顺序。这种方法无需外部预训练编码器（如DINO），且可学习基比固定基更好地适应数据统计特性。

Result: 在ImageNet-256上，Frequency-Forcing在FID指标上持续优于强像素空间和潜空间基线，且能与语义流自然组合以进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 基于引导（forcing）的尺度排序是一种灵活、路径保持的替代方案，相比硬频率约束的流模型具有更强的通用性和可组合性。

Abstract: While standard flow-matching models transport noise to data uniformly, incorporating an explicit generation order - specifically, establishing coarse, low-frequency structure before fine detail - has proven highly effective for synthesizing natural images. Two recent works offer distinct paradigms for this. K-Flow imposes a hard frequency constraint by reinterpreting a frequency scaling variable as flow time, running the trajectory inside a transformed amplitude space. Latent Forcing provides a soft ordering mechanism by coupling the pixel flow with an auxiliary semantic latent flow via asynchronous time schedules, leaving the pixel interpolation path itself untouched. Viewed from the angle of improving pixel generation, we observe that forcing - guiding generation with an earlier-maturing auxiliary stream - offers a highly compatible route to scale-ordered generation without rewriting the core flow coordinate. Building on this, we propose Frequency-Forcing, which realizes K-Flow's frequency ordering through Latent Forcing's soft mechanism: a standard pixel flow is guided by an auxiliary low-frequency stream that matures earlier in time. Unlike Latent Forcing, whose scratchpad relies on a heavy pretrained encoder (e.g., DINO), our frequency scratchpad is derived from the data itself via a lightweight learnable wavelet packet transform. We term this a self-forcing signal, which avoids external dependencies while learning a basis better adapted to data statistics than the fixed bases used in hard frequency flows. On ImageNet-256, Frequency-Forcing consistently improves FID over strong pixel- and latent-space baselines, and naturally composes with a semantic stream to yield further gains. This illustrates that forcing-based scale ordering is a versatile, path-preserving alternative to hard frequency flows.

</details>


### [150] [Do Masked Autoencoders Improve Downhole Prediction? An Empirical Study on Real Well Drilling Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20909)
*Aleksander Berezowski,Hassan Hassanzadeh,Gouri Ginde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将掩码自编码器（MAE）预训练应用于井下钻井指标预测，在 Utah FORGE 两口地热井的约 350 万时间步数据上系统搜索了 72 种 MAE 配置，并与监督式 LSTM/GRU 基线对比。最佳 MAE 相比 GRU 降低 19.8% 的 MAE，但落后 LSTM 6.4%。分析表明潜空间宽度是关键架构因素，而掩码比例因 1 Hz 钻井数据的高时间冗余而影响甚微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 井下钻井遥测存在标签不对称问题：地表数据连续密集（1 Hz），而井下标注数据成本高、稀疏且不连续。现有方法普遍采用从零开始的全监督训练，不适应这种数据分布，亟需探索更有效的预训练范式。

Method: 采用掩码自编码器（MAE）对多变量钻井遥测时间序列进行预训练，然后在 Total Mud Volume 预测任务上进行下游微调。在 Utah FORGE 两口地热井的约 350 万时间步数据上，进行了全因子实验设计，遍历 72 种 MAE 配置，并与监督式 LSTM 和 GRU 基线对比。

Result: 最佳 MAE 配置相比 GRU 基线将测试 MAE 降低 19.8%，但比 LSTM 基线高 6.4%。潜空间宽度是主导性架构选择（Pearson r = -0.59），掩码比例影响可忽略不计。

Conclusion: MAE 预训练可成为钻井分析中可行的新范式，明确了其最有利的条件，并揭示了 1 Hz 钻井时间序列数据的高时间冗余特性。

Abstract: Downhole drilling telemetry presents a fundamental labeling asymmetry: surface sensor data are generated continuously at 1~Hz, while labeled downhole measurements are costly, intermittent, and scarce. Current machine learning approaches for downhole metric prediction universally adopt fully supervised training from scratch, which is poorly suited to this data regime. We present the first empirical evaluation of masked autoencoder (MAE) pretraining for downhole drilling metric prediction. Using two publicly available Utah FORGE geothermal wells comprising approximately 3.5 million timesteps of multivariate drilling telemetry, we conduct a systematic full-factorial design space search across 72 MAE configurations and compare them against supervised LSTM and GRU baselines on the task of predicting Total Mud Volume. Results show that the best MAE configuration reduces test mean absolute error by 19.8\% relative to the supervised GRU baseline, while trailing the supervised LSTM baseline by 6.4\%. Analysis of design dimensions reveals that latent space width is the dominant architectural choice (Pearson $r = -0.59$ with test MAE), while masking ratio has negligible effect, an unexpected finding attributed to high temporal redundancy in 1~Hz drilling data. These results establish MAE pretraining as a viable paradigm for drilling analytics and identify the conditions under which it is most beneficial.

</details>


### [151] [FairyFuse: Multiplication-Free LLM Inference on CPUs via Fused Ternary Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20913)
*Fei Zuo,Xiaoyan Xi,Quanyi Zeng,Feiyu Wang,Ho Fai Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairyFuse是一个面向CPU的乘法无关推理系统，通过将三值权重({-1,0,+1})的8个子GEMV融合为单个AVX-512循环，实现16倍权重压缩，在带宽受限的CPU上获得29.6倍内核加速，端到端达到32.4 tokens/s，比llama.cpp Q4_K_M快1.24倍，且精度接近无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在纯CPU平台上部署时，内存带宽是自回归生成的主要瓶颈。现有的4比特及以下权重量化系统仍需要反量化和浮点乘法运算，限制了性能提升空间。三值权重({-1,0,+1})可以将乘法替换为条件加减法或无操作，但现有系统未能利用这一结构优势。

Method: FairyFuse通过将每个广泛线性层的8个实值子GEMV融合为一个单一的AVX-512循环，使用掩码加法和减法操作，完全消除浮点乘法。利用三值权重的结构特性，实现16倍权重压缩，并通过Roofline分析证明该方法能将内存受限的GEMV推向计算受限区域。

Result: 在Intel Xeon 8558P上实现32.4 tokens/s的端到端性能，比llama.cpp Q4_K_M快1.24倍；内核加速比达29.6倍；WikiText-2困惑度5.52（FP16为5.47），下游准确率66.0%，实现接近无损的质量。

Conclusion: FairyFuse证明了在CPU上通过三值量化和乘法无关推理可以有效加速LLM推理，16倍权重压缩能将内存瓶颈转向计算瓶颈，在带宽受限的CPU上效果显著，但在GPU上收益有限。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed on CPU-only platforms where memory bandwidth is the primary bottleneck for autoregressive generation. Weight quantization to four bits or below reduces memory pressure, yet existing systems still dequantize weights and perform floating-point multiplications, limiting the achievable gains. Ternary weights in {-1, 0, +1} provide a more efficient alternative, replacing multiplications with conditional additions, subtractions, or no-ops. While Fairy2i shows that ternary LLMs can match FP16 quality, its runtime does not exploit this structure. We present FairyFuse, an inference system that enables multiplication-free execution on commodity CPUs by fusing the eight real-valued sub-GEMVs of each widely-linear layer into a single AVX-512 loop using masked additions and subtractions, with zero floating-point multiplications. Roofline analysis shows that 16x weight compression shifts memory-bound GEMV toward the compute regime on bandwidth-limited CPUs, yielding a 29.6x kernel speedup while offering little benefit on GPUs. End-to-end, FairyFuse achieves 32.4 tokens per second on a single Intel Xeon 8558P, outperforming llama.cpp Q4_K_M by 1.24x with near-lossless quality (WikiText-2 perplexity 5.52 vs. 5.47 FP16; downstream accuracy 66.0%).

</details>


### [152] [Forget, Then Recall: Learnable Compression and Selective Unfolding via Gist Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20920)
*Yuzhen Mao,Michael Y. Li,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GSA（Gist Sparse Attention）方法，通过可学习的gist压缩令牌作为稀疏注意力的路由信号，实现粗到细的上下文选择机制，在LongBench和RAG基准上以8倍到32倍压缩率持续优于其他压缩方法和推理时稀疏注意力方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展大语言模型到长上下文面临全注意力的二次计算成本问题，现有缓解方法包括KV缓存选择或压缩技术，但缺乏有效的端到端可学习桥接方案。

Method: 核心方法为选择性展开（selective unfolding）：先通过交错放置的gist压缩令牌对原始令牌进行压缩，然后选择最相关的gist，再恢复对应的原始块进行细粒度注意力。该方法可端到端训练，并通过递归gist-of-gist构建实现层次化多分辨率上下文访问，解码复杂度为对数级别。

Result: 在LongBench和RAG基准上，GSA在8倍到32倍压缩率下持续优于其他压缩基线方法和推理时稀疏注意力方法。

Conclusion: GSA提供了一种简单有效的粗到细机制，无需架构修改或外部检索模块，即可结合紧凑全局表示与细粒度证据的目标访问，在长上下文场景中实现了性能与效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: Scaling large language models to long contexts is challenging due to the quadratic computational cost of full attention. Mitigation approaches include KV-cache selection or compression techniques. We instead provide an effective and end-to-end learnable bridge between the two without requiring architecture modification. In particular, our key insight is that interleaved gist compression tokens -- which provide a learnable summary of sets of raw tokens -- can serve as routing signals for sparse attention. Building on this, we introduce selective unfolding via GSA, which first compresses the context into gist tokens, then selects the most relevant gists, and subsequently restores the corresponding raw chunks for detailed attention. This yields a simple coarse-to-fine mechanism that combines compact global representations with targeted access to fine-grained evidence. We further incorporate this process directly into training in an end-to-end fashion, avoiding the need for external retrieval modules. In addition, we extend the framework hierarchically via recursive gist-of-gist construction, enabling multi-resolution context access with logarithmic per-step decoding complexity. Empirical results on LongBench and RAG benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms other compression baselines as well as inference-time sparse attention methods across compression ratios from $8\times$ to $32\times$. The code is available at: https://github.com/yuzhenmao/gist-sparse-attention/

</details>


### [153] [Validating a Deep Learning Algorithm to Identify Patients with Glaucoma using Systemic Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20921)
*John Xiang,Rohith Ravindranath,Sophia Y. Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于All of Us国家数据训练的青光眼风险评估（GRA）模型，仅使用系统电子健康记录（EHR）即可在独立机构中识别高风险患者，最佳模型AUROC达0.883，PPV达0.657，最高预测十分位数组的青光眼诊断率（65.7%）和治疗率（57.0%）最高，性能随可训练层数和数据量增加而提升，表明无需专业成像的EHR-only GRA模型可实现可扩展、可及的青光眼预筛查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估仅使用系统性EHR数据（无需专业眼科成像）训练的青光眼风险评估模型在独立机构中识别高风险患者的可行性，以实现可扩展、可及的青光眼预筛查。

Method: 横断面研究，纳入20,636名Stanford患者（15%患有青光眼），对预训练的GRA模型进行微调，输入特征包括人口统计学、系统性诊断、用药、实验室结果和体格检查测量值，在保留测试集上评估性能。

Result: 最佳模型AUROC 0.883，PPV 0.657；最高预测十分位数组青光眼诊断率65.7%、治疗率57.0%；性能随可训练层数（最多15层）和数据量增加而提升。

Conclusion: 基于EHR数据的GRA模型无需专业成像即可实现青光眼的可扩展、可及预筛查，校准结果与临床风险一致。

Abstract: We evaluated whether a glaucoma risk assessment (GRA) model trained on All of Us national data can identify patients at high probability of glaucoma using only systemic electronic health records (EHR) at an independent institution. In this cross-sectional study, 20,636 Stanford patients seen from November 2013 to January 2024 were included (15% with glaucoma). A pretrained GRA model was fine-tuned on the Stanford cohort and tested on a held-out set using demographics, systemic diagnoses, medications, laboratory results, and physical examination measurements as inputs. The best model achieved AUROC 0.883 and PPV 0.657. Calibration was consistent with clinical risk: the highest prediction decile showed the greatest glaucoma diagnosis rate (65.7%) and treatment rate (57.0%). Performance improved with more trainable layers up to 15 and with additional data. An EHR-only GRA model may enable scalable and accessible pre-screening without specialized imaging.

</details>


### [154] [ILDR: Geometric Early Detection of Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20923)
*Shreel Golwala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出类间/类内距离比（ILDR），一种基于倒数第二层表征的几何度量，可早于验证集准确率提升检测到grokking现象的发生，为延迟泛化提供早期信号，并支持提前停止和优化器干预等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有grokking检测信号存在间接性或不稳定性：权重范数滞后于泛化转变，GrokFast的慢梯度EMA在不同种子间波动大（标准差超过均值提前时间）。需要一种更直接、稳定且早于转变出现的检测信号。

Method: 提出ILDR（Inter/Intra-class Distance Ratio），计算倒数第二层表征中类间质心距离与类内散度的比值，基于Fisher线性判别准则，无需特征分解，计算复杂度O(|C|^2 + N)，仅在留出数据上评估以抵抗记忆化影响。

Result: ILDR在验证准确率提升前越过阈值（基线的2.5倍），提前量占训练预算的9%-73%（随任务代数复杂度增加而增大）；8个种子下提前950±250步，变异系数26%；后grokking方差降低1696倍；作为早停触发器平均减少18.6%训练；优化器干预可双向控制转变。

Conclusion: ILDR追踪的是泛化背后的表征条件（几何重组）而非下游关联指标，能够提供早期、稳定、可干预的grokking检测信号。

Abstract: Grokking describes a delayed generalization phenomenon in which a neural network achieves perfect training accuracy long before validation accuracy improves, followed by an abrupt transition to strong generalization. Existing detection signals are indirect: weight norm reflects parameter-space regularization and consistently lags the transition, while GrokFast's slow gradient EMA, used without gradient amplification, is unstable across seeds with standard deviation exceeding mean lead time. We propose the Inter/Intra-class Distance Ratio (ILDR), a geometric metric computed on second-to-last layer representations as the ratio of inter-class centroid separation to intra-class scatter. ILDR provides an early detection signal: it rises and crosses a threshold at 2.5 times its baseline before the grokking transition appears in validation accuracy, indicating early geometric reorganization in representation space. Grounded in Fisher's linear discriminant criterion, ILDR requires no eigendecomposition and runs in O(|C|^2 + N). It is evaluated exclusively on held-out data, making it robust to memorization effects. Across modular arithmetic and permutation group composition (S5), ILDR leads the grokking transition by 9 to 73 percent of the training budget, with lead time increasing with task algebraic complexity. Over eight random seeds, ILDR leads by 950 +/- 250 steps with a coefficient of variation of 26 percent, and post-grokking variance drops by 1696 times, consistent with a sharp phase transition in representation space. Using ILDR as an early stopping trigger reduces training by 18.6 percent on average. Optimizer interventions triggered at the ILDR threshold demonstrate bidirectional control over the transition, suggesting ILDR tracks representational conditions underlying generalization rather than a downstream correlate.

</details>


### [155] [Clinically Interpretable Sepsis Early Warning via LLM-Guided Simulation of Temporal Physiological Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20924)
*Weizhi Nie,Zhen Qu,Weijie Wang,Chunpei Li,Ke Lu,Bingyang Zhou,Hongzhi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种LLM引导的时间模拟框架，通过显式建模脓毒症发病前的生理轨迹实现早期可解释预警，在MIMIC-IV和eICU数据集上AUC达0.861-0.903，优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的脓毒症预警模型虽准确率高但缺乏可解释性，限制了临床医生的信任和实际应用；亟需一种既能保持高性能又能提供透明预测机制的解决方案。

Method: 框架包含三个核心模块：1) 时空特征提取模块，捕获多变量生命体征的动态依赖关系；2) 医学提示作为前缀模块，将临床推理线索嵌入到LLM中；3) 基于智能体的后处理组件，将预测约束在生理合理范围内。先模拟关键生理指标演化，再分类脓毒症发作。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和eICU数据库上，24-4小时提前预测任务的AUC为0.861-0.903，优于传统深度学习和基于规则的方法。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了可解释的轨迹和风险趋势，有助于临床医生在ICU环境中进行早期干预和个性化决策。

Abstract: Timely and interpretable early warning of sepsis remains a major clinical challenge due to the complex temporal dynamics of physiological deterioration. Traditional data-driven models often provide accurate yet opaque predictions, limiting physicians' confidence and clinical applicability. To address this limitation, we propose a Large Language Model (LLM)-guided temporal simulation framework that explicitly models physiological trajectories prior to disease onset for clinically interpretable prediction. The framework consists of a spatiotemporal feature extraction module that captures dynamic dependencies among multivariate vital signs, a Medical Prompt-as-Prefix module that embeds clinical reasoning cues into LLMs, and an agent-based post-processing component that constrains predictions within physiologically plausible ranges. By first simulating the evolution of key physiological indicators and then classifying sepsis onset, our model offers transparent prediction mechanisms that align with clinical judgment. Evaluated on the MIMIC-IV and eICU databases, the proposed method achieves superior AUC scores (0.861-0.903) across 24-4-hour pre-onset prediction tasks, outperforming conventional deep learning and rule-based approaches. More importantly, it provides interpretable trajectories and risk trends that can assist clinicians in early intervention and personalized decision-making in intensive care environments.

</details>


### [156] [Domain-Aware Hierarchical Contrastive Learning for Semi-Supervised Generalization Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20928)
*Junyu Ren,Wensheng Gan,Philip S Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向半监督域泛化故障诊断的统一框架DAHCL，通过域感知学习校准伪标签偏差，结合分层对比学习实现不确定样本的有效利用，在三个基准数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督域泛化故障诊断方法存在两个耦合问题：1）伪标签生成忽略域间几何差异导致系统性跨域偏差；2）对无标注样本采用硬性接受或丢弃策略，导致样本利用不均衡且引入额外噪声。

Method: 提出DAHCL框架，包含两个核心模块：1）域感知学习模块，显式捕捉源域几何特征并校准跨源域的伪标签预测；2）分层对比学习模块，结合动态置信度分层与模糊对比监督，使不确定样本也能参与表征学习。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上进行了大量实验，并在评估协议中加入了工程噪声以更贴近实际工业场景，实验结果显示DAHCL优于现有方法。

Conclusion: DAHCL通过域感知学习缓解伪标签偏差，利用分层对比学习提升无标注样本利用率，为半监督域泛化故障诊断提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis under unseen operating conditions remains highly challenging when labeled data are scarce. Semi-supervised domain generalization fault diagnosis (SSDGFD) provides a practical solution by jointly exploiting labeled and unlabeled source domains. However, existing methods still suffer from two coupled limitations. First, pseudo-labels for unlabeled domains are typically generated primarily from knowledge learned on the labeled source domain, which neglects domain-specific geometric discrepancies and thus induces systematic cross-domain pseudo-label bias. Second, unlabeled samples are commonly handled with a hard accept-or-discard strategy, where rigid thresholding causes imbalanced sample utilization across domains, while hard-label assignment for uncertain samples can easily introduce additional noise. To address these issues, we propose a unified framework termed domain-aware hierarchical contrastive learning (DAHCL) for SSDGFD. Specifically, DAHCL introduces a domain-aware learning (DAL) module to explicitly capture source-domain geometric characteristics and calibrate pseudo-label predictions across heterogeneous source domains, thereby mitigating cross-domain bias in pseudo-label generation. In addition, DAHCL develops a hierarchical contrastive learning (HCL) module that combines dynamic confidence stratification with fuzzy contrastive supervision, enabling uncertain samples to contribute to representation learning without relying on unreliable hard labels. In this way, DAHCL jointly improves the quality of supervision and the utilization of unlabeled samples. Furthermore, to better reflect practical industrial scenarios, we incorporate engineering noise into the SSDGFD evaluation protocol. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that...

</details>


### [157] [Data-Driven Open-Loop Simulation for Digital-Twin Operator Decision Support in Wastewater Treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20935)
*Gary Simethy,Daniel Ortiz Arroyo,Petar Durdevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CCSS-RS的受控连续时间状态空间模型，用于 wastewater treatment plants 的数字孪生决策支持。该模型在Avedøre全尺寸基准上（906,815时间步，43%缺失率，1-20分钟不规则采样）实现了RMSE 0.696和CRPS 0.349（H=1000），相比Neural CDE基线降低了40-46%的RMSE，并通过四个案例研究证明了其操作价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 污水处理厂需要能够模拟受控计划下工厂响应的数字孪生决策支持工具，且需容忍不规律和缺失的传感数据，并在12-36小时规划范围内保持信息有效性。基于全规模工厂数据满足这些需求仍是一个开放的工程-AI挑战。

Method: 提出CCSS-RS（受控连续时间状态空间模型），将历史状态推断与未来控制和外部输入展开分离。模型包括：类型化上下文编码、增益加权的规定和预测驱动强迫、半群一致的展开，以及用于处理重尾和零膨胀WWTP传感器数据的Student-t加hurdle输出分布。

Result: 在Avedøre基准上，模型在H=1000时达到RMSE 0.696和CRPS 0.349，相比Neural CDE基线降低40-46% RMSE，相比简化内部变体降低31-35% RMSE。四个案例研究显示：氧设定点扰动使预测铵变化-2.3到+1.4；平滑设定点计划在多准则筛选中排名第一；仅上下文传感器缺失使监测变量RMSE最多增加10%；铵、硝酸盐和氧在展开过程中始终优于持久性方法。

Conclusion: CCSS-RS被确立为一种实用的学习型模拟器，可用于工业废水处理中的离线场景筛选，与机理模型形成互补。

Abstract: Wastewater treatment plants (WWTPs) need digital-twin-style decision support tools that can simulate plant response under prescribed control plans, tolerate irregular and missing sensing, and remain informative over 12-36 h planning horizons. Meeting these requirements with full-scale plant data remains an open engineering-AI challenge. We present CCSS-RS, a controlled continuous-time state-space model that separates historical state inference from future control and exogenous rollout. The model combines typed context encoding, gain-weighted forcing of prescribed and forecast drivers, semigroup-consistent rollouts, and Student-t plus hurdle outputs for heavy-tailed and zero-inflated WWTP sensor data. On the public Avedøre full-scale benchmark, with 906,815 timesteps, 43% missingness, and 1-20 min irregular sampling, CCSS-RS achieves RMSE 0.696 and CRPS 0.349 at H=1000 across 10,000 test windows. This reduces RMSE by 40-46% relative to Neural CDE baselines and by 31-35% relative to simplified internal variants. Four case studies using a frozen checkpoint on test data demonstrate operational value: oxygen-setpoint perturbations shift predicted ammonium by -2.3 to +1.4 over horizons 300-1000; a smoothed setpoint plan ranks first in multi-criterion screening; context-only sensor outages raise monitored-variable RMSE by at most 10%; and ammonium, nitrate, and oxygen remain more accurate than persistence throughout the rollout. These results establish CCSS-RS as a practical learned simulator for offline scenario screening in industrial wastewater treatment, complementary to mechanistic models.

</details>


### [158] [Sink-Token-Aware Pruning for Fine-Grained Video Understanding in Efficient Video LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20937)
*Kibum Kim,Jiwan Kim,Kyle Min,Yueqi Wang,Jinyoung Moon,Julian McAuley,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示现有视频大模型视觉令牌剪枝方法在细粒度理解任务上性能严重下降，归因于"沉没令牌"问题，并提出SToP方法通过沉没分数抑制此类令牌，在剪枝90%视觉令牌时仍显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关的视觉令牌剪枝方法主要在MCQA基准上验证，忽略了细粒度理解任务；研究发现这些方法在幻觉评估等需要精确视觉定位的任务上性能严重下降，而"沉没令牌"是导致该问题的关键障碍。

Method: 提出SToP（Sink-Token-aware Pruning）方法，引入沉没分数量化每个令牌成为沉没令牌的倾向，并将该分数应用于现有的空间和时间剪枝方法中抑制沉没令牌，从而增强视频理解。

Result: 将SToP应用于VisionZip、FastVid和Holitom等SOTA剪枝方法，在幻觉评估、开放生成、组合推理和MCQA等多种基准上验证，即使剪枝90%视觉令牌仍能显著提升性能。

Conclusion: SToP作为一种即插即用的简单方法，有效解决了沉没令牌对细粒度视频理解的负面影响，大幅提升了高压缩率下的视觉令牌剪枝效果。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models (Video LLMs) incur high inference latency due to a large number of visual tokens provided to LLMs. To address this, training-free visual token pruning has emerged as a solution to reduce computational costs; however, existing methods are primarily validated on Multiple-Choice Question Answering (MCQA) benchmarks, where coarse-grained cues often suffice. In this work, we reveal that these methods suffer a sharp performance collapse on fine-grained understanding tasks requiring precise visual grounding, such as hallucination evaluation. To explore this gap, we conduct a systematic analysis and identify sink tokens--semantically uninformative tokens that attract excessive attention--as a key obstacle to fine-grained video understanding. When these sink tokens survive pruning, they distort the model's visual evidence and hinder fine-grained understanding. Motivated by these insights, we propose Sink-Token-aware Pruning (SToP), a simple yet effective plug-and-play method that introduces a sink score to quantify each token's tendency to behave as a sink and applies this score to existing spatial and temporal pruning methods to suppress them, thereby enhancing video understanding. To validate the effectiveness of SToP, we apply it to state-of-the-art pruning methods (VisionZip, FastVid, and Holitom) and evaluate it across diverse benchmarks covering hallucination, open-ended generation, compositional reasoning, and MCQA. Our results demonstrate that SToP significantly boosts performance, even when pruning up to 90% of visual tokens.

</details>


### [159] [SCM: Sleep-Consolidated Memory with Algorithmic Forgetting for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20943)
*Saish Sachin Shinde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCM（睡眠整合记忆）架构，受神经科学启发，为大语言模型设计了包含工作记忆、重要性标记、NREM/REM睡眠阶段整合、遗忘机制和自我模型的记忆系统。在8项基准测试中实现十轮对话完美召回，记忆噪声降低90.9%，搜索延迟低于1毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆系统存在根本性局限：依赖上下文窗口截断、无界增长的向量数据库、缺乏整合与遗忘机制的分层存储。本文受人类记忆神经科学原理启发，旨在建立持久、结构化、生物可行的记忆架构。

Method: SCM架构包含五个核心组件：1）有限容量工作记忆；2）多维度重要性标记；3）离线睡眠阶段整合（NREM与REM阶段分离）；4）基于意图价值的遗忘机制；5）计算自我模型支持内省。通过标准化基准套件（8项测试）评估性能。

Result: 原型系统在十轮对话中实现完美召回准确率；通过自适应遗忘将记忆噪声降低90.9%；即使存储数百个概念，记忆搜索延迟仍低于1毫秒。

Conclusion: 该工作为具备整合、优先级排序和遗忘能力的记忆系统建立了架构基础，为推进LLM记忆研究提供了可测试的平台。

Abstract: We present SCM (Sleep-Consolidated Memory), a research preview of a memory architecture for large language models that draws on neuroscientific principles to address a fundamental limitation in current systems: the absence of persistent, structured, and biologically plausible memory. Existing approaches rely on truncating context windows, growing vector databases without bound, or tiered storage systems that lack consolidation and forgetting mechanisms. SCM implements five core components inspired by human memory: a limited-capacity working memory, multi-dimensional importance tagging, offline sleep-stage consolidation with distinct NREM and REM phases, intentional value-based forgetting, and a computational self-model enabling introspection. Across a standardized benchmark suite of eight tests, the prototype achieves perfect recall accuracy over ten-turn conversations while reducing memory noise by 90.9% through adaptive forgetting. Memory search latency remains below one millisecond even with hundreds of stored concepts. This work establishes the architectural foundations for memory systems that consolidate, prioritize, and forget, offering a testable platform for advancing LLM memory research.

</details>


### [160] [LAF-Based Evaluation and UTTL-Based Learning Strategies with MIATTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20944)
*Yongquan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了EL-MIATTs框架下的两种互补机制：基于LAF（逻辑评估公式）的评估算法和基于UTTL（未定义真实目标学习）的学习策略，用于处理真实目标无法精确定义的机器学习场景，并弥合逻辑语义与统计优化之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际机器学习应用中，由于模糊性（ambiguity）或主观性（subjectivity），真实目标无法精确定义，传统的"真实标签"假设不再成立。为此，需要一种能够在不确定监督下实现逻辑连贯且实际可行的建模框架。

Method: 1) 分析任务特定的MIATTs（多个不准确真实目标），考察其覆盖度和多样性如何决定结构特性及对下游评估和学习的影响；2) 提出基于LAF的评估算法，可操作于原始MIATTs或由它们合成的三元目标，平衡可解释性、合理性和完备性；3) 提出基于UTTL的学习策略，使用Dice损失和交叉熵损失，比较了逐目标优化和聚合优化方案；4) 探讨LAF和UTTL的整合如何弥合逻辑语义与统计优化之间的鸿沟。

Result: 开发了两种互补机制（LAF评估算法和UTTL学习策略），实现了EL-MIATTs框架的理论与实践衔接，为在"真实标签"本质上不确定的场景下开发机器学习系统提供了原则性基础。

Conclusion: LAF和UTTL的整合为实施EL-MIATTs提供了连贯的路径，使得在真实目标不存在客观实体的情况下，机器学习系统仍能进行逻辑连贯且实际可行的建模。

Abstract: In many real-world machine learning (ML) applications, the true target cannot be precisely defined due to ambiguity or subjectivity information. To address this challenge, under the assumption that the true target for a given ML task is not assumed to exist objectively in the real world, the EL-MIATTs (Evaluation and Learning with Multiple Inaccurate True Targets) framework has been proposed. Bridging theory and practice in implementing EL-MIATTs, in this paper, we develop two complementary mechanisms: LAF (Logical Assessment Formula)-based evaluation algorithms and UTTL (Undefinable True Target Learning)-based learning strategies with MIATTs, which together enable logically coherent and practically feasible modeling under uncertain supervision. We first analyze task-specific MIATTs, examining how their coverage and diversity determine their structural property and influence downstream evaluation and learning. Based on this understanding, we formulate LAF-grounded evaluation algorithms that operate either on original MIATTs or on ternary targets synthesized from them, balancing interpretability, soundness, and completeness. For model training, we introduce UTTL-grounded learning strategies using Dice and cross-entropy loss functions, comparing per-target and aggregated optimization schemes. We also discuss how the integration of LAF and UTTL bridges the gap between logical semantics and statistical optimization. Together, these components provide a coherent pathway for implementing EL-MIATTs, offering a principled foundation for developing ML systems in scenarios where the notion of "ground truth" is inherently uncertain. An application of this work's results is presented as part of the study available at https://www.qeios.com/read/EZWLSN.

</details>


### [161] [Differentially Private Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20985)
*Qichuan Yin,Manzil Zaheer,Tian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种后处理技术（随机选择和线性组合），利用一组已在同一数据集上训练好的、具有不同隐私/效用权衡的模型，无需额外训练即可生成满足任意目标差分隐私（DP）要求的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器学习应用中，推理或部署时的隐私要求可能因政策、法规或用户体验的变化而动态调整，现有方法通常需要为每个隐私需求重新训练模型，成本高昂。

Method: 提出两种后处理技术：随机选择和线性组合。从Rényi差分隐私和隐私损失分布的角度分析隐私核算，并在私有均值估计案例中完整刻画隐私/效用结果。

Result: 理论上证明了线性组合优于随机选择；在私有均值估计案例中给出完整的隐私/效用刻画；在合成和真实数据集上通过多种模型验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 利用现有模型集合，通过简单的后处理操作即可灵活满足任意目标DP需求，无需重新训练，为动态隐私需求的部署提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: In machine learning applications, privacy requirements during inference or deployment time could change constantly due to varying policies, regulations, or user experience. In this work, we aim to generate a magnitude of models to satisfy any target differential privacy (DP) requirement without additional training steps, given a set of existing models trained on the same dataset with different privacy/utility tradeoffs. We propose two post processing techniques, namely random selection and linear combination, to output a final private model for any target privacy parameter. We provide privacy accounting of these approaches from the lens of R'enyi DP and privacy loss distributions for general problems. In a case study on private mean estimation, we fully characterize the privacy/utility results and theoretically establish the superiority of linear combination over random selection. Empirically, we validate our approach and analyses on several models and both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [162] [SGD at the Edge of Stability: The Stochastic Sharpness Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21016)
*Fangshuo Liao,Afroditi Kolomvaki,Anastasios Kyrillidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出随机自稳定机制，将边缘稳定性理论从全批量梯度下降扩展到小批量随机梯度下降。通过分析梯度噪声在顶部Hessian特征向量上的投影方差，推导出均衡锐度间隙的闭式公式ΔS = ηβσ_u²/(4α)，解释了为什么小批量SGD比GD收敛到更平坦的解（锐度更低），且批大小越小，解越平坦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全批量GD中锐度稳定在2/η的现象（边缘稳定性EoS）已由自稳定机制解释，但小批量SGD中锐度稳定在低于2/η且批大小越小间隙越大的现象缺乏理论解释。本文旨在将自稳定框架扩展到SGD，解释梯度噪声如何抑制锐度。

Method: 1. 将自稳定框架从GD扩展到SGD，引入随机自稳定概念；2. 定义相对于移动投影梯度下降轨迹的随机预测动力学；3. 证明随机耦合定理，界定了SGD与预测轨迹的偏差；4. 推导均衡锐度间隙的闭式公式ΔS = ηβσ_u²/(4α)，其中α为渐进锐化率，β为自稳定强度，σ_u²为梯度噪声在顶部特征向量上的投影方差。

Result: 推导出SGD均衡锐度低于GD的定量关系：ΔS = ηβσ_u²/(4α)。该公式预测小批大小产生更平坦的解，当批大小等于完整数据集时恢复GD的结果。

Conclusion: 小批量SGD的锐度抑制机制源于梯度噪声在顶部Hessian特征向量方向上的方差注入，增强了三次锐度缩减力，从而将均衡锐度从2/η下移。这为理解深度学习中小批量的隐式正则化效应提供了理论依据。

Abstract: When training neural networks with full-batch gradient descent (GD) and step size $η$, the largest eigenvalue of the Hessian -- the sharpness $S(\boldsymbolθ)$ -- rises to $2/η$ and hovers there, a phenomenon termed the Edge of Stability (EoS). \citet{damian2023selfstab} showed that this behavior is explained by a self-stabilization mechanism driven by third-order structure of the loss, and that GD implicitly follows projected gradient descent (PGD) on the constraint $ S(\boldsymbolθ)\leq 2/η$. For mini-batch stochastic gradient descent (SGD), the sharpness stabilizes below $2/η$, with the gap widening as the batch size decreases; yet no theoretical explanation exists for this suppression.
  We introduce stochastic self-stabilization, extending the self-stabilization framework to SGD. Our key insight is that gradient noise injects variance into the oscillatory dynamics along the top Hessian eigenvector, strengthening the cubic sharpness-reducing force and shifting the equilibrium below $2/η$. Following the approach of \citet{damian2023selfstab}, we define stochastic predicted dynamics relative to a moving projected gradient descent trajectory and prove a stochastic coupling theorem that bounds the deviation of SGD from these predictions. We derive a closed-form equilibrium sharpness gap: $ΔS = ηβσ_{\boldsymbol{u}}^{2}/(4α)$, where $α$ is the progressive sharpening rate, $β$ is the self-stabilization strength, and $σ_{ \boldsymbol{u}}^{2}$ is the gradient noise variance projected onto the top eigenvector. This formula predicts that smaller batch sizes yield flatter solutions and recovers GD when the batch equals the full dataset.

</details>


### [163] [MCAP: Deployment-Time Layer Profiling for Memory-Constrained LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21026)
*Anurita Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MCAP是一种负载时每层重要性估计方法，通过动态精度分配（W4A8 vs W4A16）和内存层级调度（GPU/RAM/SSD）实现单套权重适应不同内存预算；其系统NVE在NVIDIA T4上相比llama.cpp Q4_0提升1.5-1.8倍解码吞吐量，使模型能在无需修改权重的情况下运行于此前不可行的内存场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署到异构硬件时，性能瓶颈通常来自内存限制而非计算能力，现有方案无法在没有权重修改的情况下适应不同内存预算的异构设备。

Method: 提出MCAP（蒙特卡洛激活分析），一种在模型加载时执行的逐层重要性估计方法，基于该信号驱动精度调度（W4A8/W4A16）和驻留层级选择（GPU/RAM/SSD），使单套权重可跨不同内存预算运行。

Result: 在NVIDIA T4上，NVE系统相比llama.cpp Q4_0实现1.5-1.8倍更高的解码吞吐量，且能在之前不可行的内存限制下运行模型。

Conclusion: MCAP结合NVE系统有效解决了异构硬件部署中的内存瓶颈问题，通过动态精度和内存管理显著提升吞吐量并拓展模型运行的内存可行性边界，且无需修改权重。

Abstract: Deploying large language models to heterogeneous hardware is often constrained by memory, not compute. We introduce MCAP (Monte Carlo Activation Profiling), a load-time per-layer importance estimator that enables dynamic precision and memory placement decisions on the target device. MCAP produces a lightweight per-layer signal that drives both precision dispatch (W4A8 vs. W4A16) and residency tier (GPU, RAM, SSD), allowing a single set of weights to operate across diverse memory budgets. Our system, NVE, achieves 1.5-1.8x higher decode throughput than llama.cpp Q4_0 on NVIDIA T4 and enables models to run in memory regimes previously infeasible without modifying weights.

</details>


### [164] [A Deep U-Net Framework for Flood Hazard Mapping Using Hydraulic Simulations of the Wupper Catchment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21028)
*Christian Lammers,Fernando Arévalo,Leonie Märker-Neuhaus,Daniel Heinenberg,Christian Förster,Karl-Heinz Spies*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的替代模型（U-Net架构），用于高效预测洪水最大水位，在德国Wupper流域的测试中取得了与传统水力模拟相当的结果，但计算效率显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球洪水事件频发且日益严重，传统水力模拟计算成本高昂，亟需开发快速可靠的洪水预测工具。

Method: 通过优化U-Net架构、分块生成和数据处理的系列实验，构建深度学习替代模型来近似水力模型，预测网格上的最大水位。

Result: 该框架在德国Wupper流域的水力模拟测试中获得了与传统方法可比的结果，同时计算效率大幅提升。

Conclusion: 深度学习替代模型可以作为传统水力模拟的计算高效替代方案，用于快速洪水预测。

Abstract: The increasing frequency and severity of global flood events highlights the need for the development of rapid and reliable flood prediction tools. This process traditionally relies on computationally expensive hydraulic simulations. This research presents a prediction tool by developing a deep-learning based surrogate model to accurately and efficiently predict the maximum water level across a grid. This was achieved by conducting a series of experiments to optimize a U-Net architecture, patch generation, and data handling for approximating a hydraulic model. This research demonstrates that a deep learning surrogate model can serve as a computationally efficient alternative to traditional hydraulic simulations. The framework was tested using hydraulic simulations of the Wupper catchment in the North-Rhein Westphalia region (Germany), obtaining comparable results.

</details>


### [165] [Interpretable Quantile Regression by Optimal Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21042)
*Valentin Lemaire,Gaël Aglin,Siegfried Nijssen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种学习最优分位数回归树集合的新方法，能在不牺牲算法效率的前提下，提供可解释的、无需先验假设的完整条件分布预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习领域对模型的准确性、可解释性和鲁棒性需求日益增长，需要一种既能提供完整条件分布预测又具备可解释性的方法。

Method: 提出一种学习最优分位数回归树集合的新方法，该方法在保持与单棵决策树相当的算法效率的同时，学习一组最优的分位数回归树。

Result: 该方法能够：1）无需先验分布假设即可预测目标变量的完整条件分布；2）提供可解释的预测结果；3）在不牺牲算法效率的前提下学习最优分位数回归树集合。

Conclusion: 该方法在准确性、可解释性和算法效率之间取得了良好平衡，为构建可理解、可信赖的AI系统提供了有效途径。

Abstract: The field of machine learning is subject to an increasing interest in models that are not only accurate but also interpretable and robust, thus allowing their end users to understand and trust AI systems. This paper presents a novel method for learning a set of optimal quantile regression trees. The advantages of this method are that (1) it provides predictions about the complete conditional distribution of a target variable without prior assumptions on this distribution; (2) it provides predictions that are interpretable; (3) it learns a set of optimal quantile regression trees without compromising algorithmic efficiency compared to learning a single tree.

</details>


### [166] [JEPAMatch: Geometric Representation Shaping for Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21046)
*Ali Aghababaei-Harandi,Aude Sportisse,Massih-Reza Amini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的半监督学习方法，将FlexMatch的损失函数与LeJEPA的潜空间各向同性高斯正则化相结合，通过显式塑造几何表示来缓解FixMatch类方法中的类别不平衡和早期伪标签噪声问题，在多个基准数据集上取得更优性能并加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FixMatch等基于伪标签的半监督方法存在两大瓶颈：多数类主导学习过程且被错误伪标签放大导致模型偏差，以及早期噪声伪标签阻碍决策边界形成、需要长时间训练才能学习到有效表示。

Method: 将FlexMatch中的经典半监督损失与LeJEPA（Latent-Euclidean Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures）导出的潜空间正则化项相结合，用各向同性高斯结构约束潜空间几何表示，实现从逻辑输出阈值到显式几何形状塑造的范式转变。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、STL-10和Tiny-ImageNet上的广泛实验表明，该方法持续优于现有基线，同时显著加速收敛，大幅降低相比标准FixMatch管线的整体计算成本。

Conclusion: 所提出的潜空间正则化策略能鼓励良好结构的表示，同时保留伪标签策略的优势，有效解决了半监督学习中的偏差和收敛慢问题。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning has emerged as a powerful paradigm for leveraging large amounts of unlabeled data to improve the performance of machine learning models when labeled data are scarce. Among existing approaches, methods derived from FixMatch have achieved state-of-the-art results in image classification by combining weak and strong data augmentations with confidence-based pseudo-labeling. Despite their strong empirical performance, these methods typically struggle with two critical bottlenecks: majority classes tend to dominate the learning process, which is amplified by incorrect pseudo-labels, leading to biased models. Furthermore, noisy early pseudo-labels prevent the model from forming clear decision boundaries, requiring prolonged training to learn informative representation. In this paper, we introduce a paradigm shift from conventional logical output threshold base, toward an explicit shaping of geometric representations. Our approach is inspired by the recently proposed Latent-Euclidean Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (LeJEPA), a theoretically grounded framework asserting that meaningful representations should exhibit an isotropic Gaussian structure in latent space. Building on this principle, we propose a new training objective that combines the classical semi-supervised loss used in FlexMatch, an adaptive extension of FixMatch, with a latent-space regularization term derived from LeJEPA.
  Our proposed approach, encourages well-structured representations while preserving the advantages of pseudo-labeling strategies. Through extensive experiments on CIFAR-100, STL-10 and Tiny-ImageNet, we demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms existing baselines. In addition, our method significantly accelerates the convergence, drastically reducing the overall computational cost compared to standard FixMatch-based pipelines.

</details>


### [167] [Preconditioned DeltaNet: Curvature-aware Sequence Modeling for Linear Recurrences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21100)
*Neehal Tumma,Noel Loo,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对现有delta规则递归（如Mamba-2、DeltaNet、GDN、KDA）仅作为在线最小二乘一阶近似、忽略损失曲率的问题，引入预条件技术（对角近似），推导出线性注意力与delta规则在精确预条件下的等价关系，并提出预条件化的DeltaNet、GDN和KDA变体及其高效分块并行算法。实验表明，预条件delta规则递归在合成召回基准和340M/1B规模语言建模上均有一致性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有delta规则递归（如DeltaNet、GDN、KDA）可被测试时回归（TTR）框架解释为在线最小二乘更新的一阶近似，但忽略了最小二乘损失函数的曲率信息，限制了模型表达能力和优化效率。

Method: 1) 从在线最小二乘理论出发，推导线性注意力与delta规则在精确预条件下的等价关系；2) 提出对角近似预条件，实现可计算的预条件化delta规则递归；3) 分别为DeltaNet、GDN、KDA设计预条件变体及其高效分块并行算法。

Result: 在合成召回基准和340M/1B参数规模的语言建模任务上，预条件delta规则递归相比原始版本取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 引入预条件（尤其对角近似）能有效弥补delta规则递归忽略曲率信息的不足，为长上下文递归算子提供更优的优化路径，且分块并行算法保证了实际可部署性。

Abstract: To address the increasing long-context compute limitations of softmax attention, several subquadratic recurrent operators have been developed. This work includes models such as Mamba-2, DeltaNet, Gated DeltaNet (GDN), and Kimi Delta Attention (KDA). As the space of recurrences grows, a parallel line of work has arisen to taxonomize them. One compelling view is the test-time regression (TTR) framework, which interprets recurrences as performing online least squares updates that learn a linear map from the keys to values. Existing delta-rule recurrences can be seen as first-order approximations to this objective, but notably ignore the curvature of the least-squares loss during optimization. In this work, we address this by introducing preconditioning to these recurrences. Starting from the theory of online least squares, we derive equivalences between linear attention and the delta rule in the exactly preconditioned case. Next, we realize this theory in practice by proposing a diagonal approximation: this enables us to introduce preconditioned variants of DeltaNet, GDN, and KDA alongside efficient chunkwise parallel algorithms for computing them. Empirically, we find that our preconditioned delta-rule recurrences yield consistent performance improvements across synthetic recall benchmarks and language modeling at the 340M and 1B scale.

</details>


### [168] [How Much Is One Recurrence Worth? Iso-Depth Scaling Laws for Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21106)
*Kristian Schwethelm,Daniel Rueckert,Georgios Kaissis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过116次预训练实验，拟合出循环语言模型的缩放定律，发现循环次数与等效参数之间的指数φ=0.46，即每增加一次循环相当于增加约0.46倍的参数效果，但训练成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化深度循环（循环）语言模型中，增加一次循环在验证损失上相当于多少等效的唯一参数，为循环架构的设计选择提供可预测的代价度量。

Method: 对116次不同循环次数（r∈{1,2,4,8}）的预训练实验进行等深度扫描，涵盖约50倍的训练计算量，拟合联合缩放定律L = E + A(N_once + r^φ N_rec)^{-α} + B D^{-β}，恢复出循环等效指数φ。

Result: 得到φ=0.46（R²=0.997），表明每增加一次循环的收益介于全等效（φ=1）和无容量增益（φ=0）之间。例如4次循环的4.1亿参数模型性能堪比5.8亿非循环模型，但训练成本相当于10亿非循环模型。下游评估中，参数知识任务差距持续存在，简单开放书任务差距缩小，推理任务在计算预算内无法解决。

Conclusion: φ=0.46将循环次数选择转换为可预测的验证损失代价，未来的训练方案和架构可以通过它们将φ提升到0.46以上的程度进行比较。

Abstract: We measure how much one extra recurrence is worth to a looped (depth-recurrent) language model, in equivalent unique parameters. From an iso-depth sweep of 116 pretraining runs across recurrence counts $r \in \{1, 2, 4, 8\}$ spanning ${\sim}50\times$ in training compute, we fit a joint scaling law $L = E + A\,(N_\text{once} + r^{\varphi} N_\text{rec})^{-α} + B\,D^{-β}$ and recover a new recurrence-equivalence exponent $\varphi = 0.46$ at $R^2 = 0.997$. Intuitively, $\varphi$ tells us whether looping a block $r$ times is equivalent in validation loss to $r$ unique blocks of a non-looped model (full equivalence, $\varphi{=}1$) or to a single block run repeatedly with no capacity gain ($\varphi{=}0$). Our $\varphi = 0.46$ sits in between, so each additional recurrence predictably increases validation loss at matched training compute. For example, at $r{=}4$ a 410M looped model performs on par with a 580M non-looped model, but pays the training cost of a 1B non-looped one. On a five-axis downstream evaluation, the gap persists on parametric-knowledge tasks and closes on simple open-book tasks, while reasoning tasks are not resolvable at our compute budgets. For any looped LM, our $\varphi$ converts the design choice of $r$ into a predictable validation-loss cost, and future training recipes and architectures can be compared by how much they raise $\varphi$ above $0.46$.

</details>


### [169] [Toward Efficient Membership Inference Attacks against Federated Large Language Models: A Projection Residual Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21197)
*Guilin Deng,Silong Chen,Yuchuan Luo,Yi Liu,Songlei Wang,Zhiping Cai,Lin Liu,Xiaohua Jia,Shaojing Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProjRes，首个针对联邦大语言模型（FedLLMs）的基于投影残差的被动成员推理攻击方法。该方法利用隐藏嵌入向量在梯度子空间上的投影残差揭示梯度与输入的内在关联，无需影子模型或辅助分类器，在四个基准和四个LLM上实现近100%的攻击准确率，比现有方法提升高达75.75%，即使在强差分隐私防御下仍然有效，揭示了FedLLMs中此前被忽视的隐私漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦大语言模型（FedLLMs）虽通过数据本地化保护隐私，但共享梯度仍可能通过成员推理攻击（MIAs）泄露敏感信息。现有MIA方法因FedLLMs的独特特性（参数量巨大、收敛快速、梯度稀疏非正交）而失效，亟需针对性的隐私攻击方法以重新审视FedLLMs的安全假设。

Method: 提出ProjRes方法，利用隐藏嵌入向量作为样本表示，分析其在梯度子空间上的投影残差，以此揭示梯度与输入数据之间的内在联系。该方法为被动攻击，无需影子模型、辅助分类器或历史更新记录。

Result: 在四个基准数据集和四个LLM上的实验表明，ProjRes达到近100%的成员推理准确率，比现有最优方法提升高达75.75%，且在强差分隐私防御下仍保持有效性。

Conclusion: ProjRes揭示了FedLLMs中此前被忽视的隐私脆弱性，呼吁重新审视其安全假设。该方法高效、鲁棒，为联邦学习隐私安全研究提供了新的视角和警示。

Abstract: Federated Large Language Models (FedLLMs) enable multiple parties to collaboratively fine-tune LLMs without sharing raw data, addressing challenges of limited resources and privacy concerns. Despite data localization, shared gradients can still expose sensitive information through membership inference attacks (MIAs). However, FedLLMs' unique properties, i.e. massive parameter scales, rapid convergence, and sparse, non-orthogonal gradients, render existing MIAs ineffective. To address this gap, we propose ProjRes, the first projection residuals-based passive MIA tailored for FedLLMs. ProjRes leverages hidden embedding vectors as sample representations and analyzes their projection residuals on the gradient subspace to uncover the intrinsic link between gradients and inputs. It requires no shadow models, auxiliary classifiers, or historical updates, ensuring efficiency and robustness. Experiments on four benchmarks and four LLMs show that ProjRes achieves near 100% accuracy, outperforming prior methods by up to 75.75%, and remains effective even under strong differential privacy defenses. Our findings reveal a previously overlooked privacy vulnerability in FedLLMs and call for a re-examination of their security assumptions. Our code and data are available at $\href{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Passive-MIA-5268}{link}$.

</details>


### [170] [ARFBench: Benchmarking Time Series Question Answering Ability for Software Incident Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21199)
*Stephan Xie,Ben Cohen,Mononito Goswami,Junhong Shen,Emaad Khwaja,Chenghao Liu,David Asker,Othmane Abou-Amal,Ameet Talwalkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARFBench是一个评估多模态基础模型在时间序列异常检测与问答能力上的基准，包含750个问题、142个时间序列和538万数据点。前沿视觉语言模型表现最好（GPT-5准确率62.7%，F1 51.9%），提出TSFM+VLM混合模型可媲美前沿模型，而模型与人类专家互补可实现超人类表现（F1 82.8%，准确率87.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列问答（TSQA）是基础模型一个有前景但探索不足的能力，现有研究缺乏专门针对软件运维数据中时间序列异常的评估基准。

Method: 构建ARFBench基准（750个问题、142个时间序列、538万数据点），评估多种多模态模型（LLM、VLM、时间序列FM），并提出TSFM+VLM混合原型模型，通过少量合成和真实数据后训练。

Result: 前沿VLM（GPT-5）准确率62.7%、F1 51.9%；TSFM+VLM混合模型与前沿模型性能相当；模型与人类专家组合的oracle选择器达到F1 82.8%、准确率87.2%，超越人类水平。

Conclusion: ARFBench为TSQA提供了标准化评估基准，揭示了模型与人类专家存在互补优势，为未来的时间序列问答模型设立了新的超人类基准。

Abstract: Time series question-answering (TSQA), in which we ask natural language questions to infer and reason about properties of time series, is a promising yet underexplored capability of foundation models. In this work, we present ARFBench, a TSQA benchmark that evaluates the understanding of multimodal foundation models (FMs) on time series anomalies prevalent in software incident data. ARFBench consists of 750 questions across 142 time series and 5.38M data points from 63 production incidents sourced exclusively from internal telemetry at Datadog. We evaluate leading proprietary and open-source LLMs, VLMs, and time series FMs and observe that frontier VLMs perform markedly better than existing baselines; the leading model (GPT-5) achieves a 62.7% accuracy and 51.9% F1. We next demonstrate the promise of specialized multimodal approaches. We develop a novel TSFM + VLM hybrid prototype which we post-train on a small set of synthetic and real data that yields comparable overall F1 and accuracy with frontier models. Lastly, we find models and human domain experts exhibit complementary strengths. We define a model-expert oracle, a best-of-2 oracle selector over model and expert answers, yielding 82.8% F1 and 87.2% accuracy and establishing a new superhuman frontier for future TSQA models. The benchmark is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Datadog/ARFBench.

</details>


### [171] [The Recurrent Transformer: Greater Effective Depth and Efficient Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21215)
*Costin-Andrei Oncescu,Depen Morwani,Samy Jelassi,Alexandru Meterez,Mujin Kwun,Sham Kakade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Recurrent Transformer架构，通过层内循环记忆机制使每个层关注自身激活产生的键值对，实现无限时间深度，同时保持标准自回归解码成本。通过基于平铺的精确算法将HBM流量从Θ(N²)降至Θ(N log N)，在150M和300M参数C4预训练中优于参数匹配的Transformer基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer在时间维度上较浅（深度受层数限制），而循环模型虽然提供无限时间深度但存在优化不稳定和加速器利用不足的问题。本文旨在结合两者优势。

Method: 提出Recurrent Transformer架构：每层关注自身激活产生的键值对，实现层间循环记忆。设计了基于平铺的精确算法，将KV缓存的内存流量从Θ(N²)降至Θ(N log N)，使算术强度从约1提升至Θ(N/log N)。

Result: 在150M和300M参数C4预训练中，Recurrent Transformer在固定参数量下使用更少层数即超越标准Transformer基线的交叉熵性能，同时降低KV缓存内存占用和推理延迟。

Conclusion: 循环机制可以用深度换取宽度，在固定参数量下减少层数，从而降低KV缓存内存占用和推理延迟，同时避免优化不稳定问题。

Abstract: Transformers process tokens in parallel but are temporally shallow: at position $t$, each layer attends to key-value pairs computed based on the previous layer, yielding a depth capped by the number of layers. Recurrent models offer unbounded temporal depth but suffer from optimization instability and historically underutilize modern accelerators. We introduce the Recurrent Transformer, a simple architectural change where each layer attends to key-value pairs computed off its own activations, yielding layerwise recurrent memory while preserving standard autoregressive decoding cost. We show that the architecture can emulate both (i) a conventional Transformer and (ii) token-to-token recurrent updates under mild assumptions, while avoiding optimization instability. Naively, prefill/training appears bandwidth-bound with effective arithmetic intensity near $1$ because keys and values are revealed sequentially; we give an exact tiling-based algorithm that preserves the mathematical computation while reducing HBM traffic from $Θ(N^2)$ to $Θ(N\log N)$, increasing effective arithmetic intensity to $Θ(N/\log N)$ for sequence length $N$. On 150M and 300M parameter C4 pretraining, Recurrent Transformers improve cross-entropy over a parameter-matched Transformer baseline and achieve the improvement with fewer layers (fixed parameters), suggesting that recurrence can trade depth for width, thus reducing KV cache memory footprint and inference latency.

</details>


### [172] [Learning Dynamic Representations and Policies from Multimodal Clinical Time-Series with Informative Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21235)
*Zihan Liang,Ziwen Pan,Ruoxuan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个多模态临床时序表征学习框架，通过显式利用信息性缺失机制（包括结构化测量和临床笔记的观测模式），结合多模态编码器、贝叶斯状态滤波器和下游任务模块，在ICU脓毒症数据集上显著提升了离线治疗策略学习和不良结局预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态临床记录包含结构化测量和临床笔记，但观测稀疏且依赖于患者潜在状态；不同模态的观测过程不同（结构化测量与临床笔记的记录机制各异）。现有方法虽能处理缺失值，但未充分利用观测过程本身携带的信息。

Method: 提出三模块框架：(1)多模态编码器——从结构数据和文本数据中提取信号并编码观测模式；(2)贝叶斯滤波模块——基于观测到的多模态信号随时间更新潜在患者状态；(3)下游模块——基于学习到的患者状态进行离线治疗策略学习和结局预测。

Result: 在MIMIC-III、MIMIC-IV和eICU的ICU脓毒症队列上评估：离线治疗策略学习FQE达0.679（对照临床行为0.528），MIMIC-III上72小时后死亡预测AUROC达0.886。

Conclusion: 显式利用观测模式（信息性缺失）能有效提升多模态临床时序数据的表征学习质量，改善治疗策略学习和预后预测性能。

Abstract: Multimodal clinical records contain structured measurements and clinical notes recorded over time, offering rich temporal information about the evolution of patient health. Yet these observations are sparse, and whether they are recorded depends on the patient's latent condition. Observation patterns also differ across modalities, as structured measurements and clinical notes arise under distinct recording processes. While prior work has developed methods that accommodate missingness in clinical time series, how to extract and use the information carried by the observation process itself remains underexplored. We therefore propose a patient representation learning framework for multimodal clinical time series that explicitly leverages informative missingness.
